Abstract: Our primary objective is to advance video object counting in crowded scenes, aiming to robustly count all instances of a target category based on given text or visual prompts. Existing methods rely on RGB information, limiting their discriminative ability in crowded and occluded conditions. To address this, we propose a Depth-Guided Detector (DG-Det) along with a general post-processing pipeline. By integrating depth cues with multi-scale RGB-D cross-attention and explicit occlusion prediction, our method enhances spatial understanding and achieves robust detection in crowded and occluded scenes. Furthermore, we introduce a unified de-duplication framework to eliminate cross-frame redundant counting. To facilitate future research, we also release a new RGB-D Video Object Counting dataset featuring depth information and multiple object categories persequence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves a 62.01\% reduction in MAE compared to existing baselines, and also produces consistent improvements in RMSE. We provide the source code at https://github.com/streamer-AP/DG-Net and the dataset at https://huggingface.co/datasets/aerospace123/RGBD-VideoCount.

</details>


### [102] [PRISM: Distribution-Gated Flow Matching for Controllable Unpaired Image Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06240)
*Elad Yoshai,Natan T. Shaked*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM是一种无GAN的流匹配框架，通过学习到的逐特征门控（per-feature gate）取代全局噪声/引导值来控制未配对图像翻译中"保留什么"与"改变什么"。门控的空间先验来自源特征到目标特征分布的标准化距离，同时控制初始化混合与ODE传输时序，并可在推理时通过文本或检测器局部覆盖，无需重训。在五个自然与生物医学基准上取得最佳或竞争力的FID/KID结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的未配对图像翻译方法使用单一全局噪声或引导值来控制图像保真度，无法在空间上区分需要保留的内容与需要改变的外观，导致翻译质量和结构保真之间难以平衡。

Method: PRISM采用无GAN的流匹配框架，核心是用一个学习到的逐特征门控替代全局控制。门控的空间先验通过每个源特征到目标特征分布的标准化距离推导（远离目标的特征被释放，与目标一致的特征被保留）。同一门控控制两处：一是初始化阶段混合真实源潜变量与任务匹配的腐蚀（content-anchored AdaIN用于保结构翻译，部分锚定用于变结构翻译），二是ODE积分时的传输时序。门控可在推理时从文本或检测器局部覆盖而无需重训。

Result: 在五个基准（AFHQ cat->dog、CelebA-HQ外观翻译、day->night重光照、虚拟染色、乳腺冰冻->永久HE病理）上，PRISM在四个基准取得最佳Inception FID和KID，在第五个取得竞争力结果；在病理学上取得最接近理想的细胞核计数比，体现目标真实感与结构保留之间的有利平衡。

Conclusion: PRISM证明了通过逐特征门控替代全局控制可以有效分离图像翻译中"保留内容"与"改变外观"这一核心问题，在自然图像与生物医学图像上都取得了优越的翻译质量与结构保真度平衡，且支持推理时的局部可控性。

Abstract: Unpaired image-to-image translation must decide, per image, what to change and what to preserve without paired supervision. Many diffusion-based unpaired translators control preservation through a single global noise or guidance value applied across the image, which cannot separate content to keep from appearance to change. We present PRISM, a GAN-free flow-matching framework that replaces this global control with a learned per-feature gate. The gate's spatial prior is derived from each source feature's standardized distance to the target feature distribution, so features far from the target are freed while target-consistent features are preserved. The same gate controls both the initialization, which mixes the real source latent with a task-matched corruption, and the transport timing during Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) integration. The corruption is matched to the task, content-anchored (AdaIN) for structure-preserving translation and partially anchored for structure-changing translation, and the gate can be overridden locally at inference from text or a detector without retraining, preserving important structures of the original image while still generating realistic results. We evaluate PRISM on five natural and biomedical benchmarks (AFHQ cat->dog, CelebA-HQ appearance translation, day->night relighting, virtual staining, and breast frozen->permanent histopathology). Among the evaluated methods under a shared same-split protocol, PRISM attains the best Inception FID and KID on four benchmarks and a competitive result on the fifth, and on histopathology yields the nuclei-count ratio closest to the ideal, supporting a favorable balance between target realism and structural preservation.

</details>


### [103] [Toward Deployable Bangla Sign Language Recognition with Expert-Validated Data and a Lightweight Attention-Based Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06252)
*Saad Ahmed,Md Khalid Syfullaha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RSBdSL38数据集（10,874张专家验证的孟加拉手语（BdSL）手部签名图像，覆盖38个手语符号/51个孟加拉字母），并设计了一个仅29.8万参数的轻量级注意力卷积网络，在从零训练下达到96.37%准确率，接近但远轻量于最快的ImageNet预训练模型，且可在手机端以0.48MB量化模型、每帧3.98ms实时运行，实现可部署的BdSL识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉聋哑人群主要通过BdSL交流，但在个人设备上实现自动BdSL识别仍缺乏高质量数据与适合端侧部署的轻量模型。现有系统依赖受控环境数据集且无专家验证，并使用不适合移动设备的重量级预训练骨干网络。

Method: 提出RSBdSL38数据集，来自孟加拉三个特殊学校的真实手语者，经专家验证含38种手部符号。设计轻量注意力卷积网络（298,470参数），包含分组瓶颈残差块、通道与空间注意力、多尺度深度可分离手部特征块、双重池化及Swish激活；从零训练并使用Grad-CAM、删除-插入及权重随机化进行可解释性验证。

Result: 从零训练达96.37%准确率（五种子平均95.72%±0.54%），比最优预训练模型低1.08个百分点，但参数减少8.5-68倍、MACs减少1.3-21.7倍。在六个公开BdSL基准上重训达92.95-98.33%，合并语料97.04%，BdSL-38零样本76.25%。消融实验显示移除任一组件损失7.61-89.30点。签者独立分割（保留6/36人）达85.18%。量化后0.48MB，手机端每帧3.98ms。

Conclusion: RSBdSL38数据集与从零训练的轻量模型共同将基准准确率转化为可部署的实际可及性，以远低于预训练骨干网络的成本实现精准的端侧BdSL识别；数据集、代码和模型均已开源发布。

Abstract: Deaf and hard-of-hearing people in Bangladesh communicate mainly through Bangla Sign Language (BdSL). Automatic BdSL recognition on personal devices could widen access to education and services. Existing systems use controlled-setting datasets without expert verification and heavyweight pretrained backbones unsuited to on-device use. We introduce RSBdSL38, 10,874 expert-validated images spanning all 38 BdSL hand signs, representing the 51 letters of the Bangla alphabet, recorded from real signers at three special-needs schools across Bangladesh. We propose a lightweight attention based convolutional network of 298,470 parameters, built from grouped bottleneck residual blocks, channel and spatial attention, a multi-scale depthwise hand-feature block, dual pooling, and Swish activations. Trained from scratch, it attains 96.37% accuracy (95.72% +- 0.54% over five seeds), within 1.08 percentage points of the best of nine ImageNet-pretrained efficient architectures under an identical protocol, using 8.5 to 68x fewer parameters and 1.3 to 21.7x fewer MACs. Retrained, it reaches 92.95 to 98.33% on six public BdSL benchmarks, 97.04% on a merged corpus, and 76.25% zero-shot on BdSL-38. Removing any architectural stage costs 7.61 to 89.30 points, against at most 3.17 for the training recipe. Grad-CAM with deletion-insertion and weight-randomization checks confirms that predictions follow the signing hand. A signer-independent split holding out 6 of 36 signers yields 85.18%. Quantized to 0.48 MB, it runs at 3.98 ms per image within a 15.5 MB footprint on a commodity smartphone. Together, RSBdSL38 and our from-scratch model turn benchmark accuracy into deployable accessibility at a fraction of pretrained-backbone cost; dataset, code, and models are released.

</details>


### [104] [HoloCount: A Holistic Visual Counting Benchmark for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06420)
*Jinhong Deng,Limeng Qiao,Guanglu Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HoloCount，一个基于三级层次分类法的综合诊断性视觉计数基准，系统评估20多个最先进MLLM在语义计数、分析计数和鲁棒性测试三个层面的计数能力，揭示了从感知到复杂推理及对抗场景中模型性能显著退化的关键差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在定性场景理解上表现出色，但定量精度（计数）仍是瓶颈，存在持续的数字幻觉问题。已有计数基准仅关注简化情境下的基础感知，未能捕捉逻辑约束或对抗条件下涌现的复杂失效模式，亟需一个更具全面性和诊断性的基准来系统评估和定位这些缺陷。

Method: 构建HoloCount基准，采用三层级层次化分类法：(1) 语义计数（原子级与属性级枚举）、(2) 分析计数（基于空间与集合推理的逻辑组合）、(3) 鲁棒性测试（高密度场景、语言偏见等对抗情境与地面反先验下探测模型完整性）。对20多个SOTA MLLM进行系统性评估。

Result: 评估结果显示存在关键的性能差距：即使顶尖模型在从感知任务转向复杂分析推理和不利场景时，性能也显著下降。该基准提供了当前MLLM计数能力的系统性图谱。

Conclusion: HoloCount为开发更接地气、更可靠的多模态系统提供了路线图，指明了模型在语义计数、分析推理与鲁棒性方面的具体短板，对推动MLLM计数能力的全面发展具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Visual counting is a fundamental pillar of multimodal intelligence, requiring a seamless integration of fine-grained grounding and spatial reasoning. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in qualitative scene understanding, their quantitative precision remains a significant bottleneck, often characterized by persistent numerical hallucinations. Existing counting benchmarks primarily focus on basic perception in simplified contexts, failing to capture the complex failure modes that emerge under logical constraints or adversarial conditions. To address these limitations, we introduce HoloCount, a holistic and diagnostically rich benchmark structured around a three-level hierarchical taxonomy. HoloCount evaluates MLLMs across: (1) Semantic Counting, focusing on atomic and property-based enumeration; (2) Analytical Counting, assessing logical composition through spatial and set-based reasoning; and (3) Robustness Testing, probing model integrity against adverse scenarios and grounded counter-priors, such as high-density scenes and linguistic biases. Through an exhaustive evaluation of over 20 state-of-the-art MLLMs, we reveal a critical performance gap: even top-tier models degrade significantly as tasks transition from perception to complex analytical reasoning and adverse scenarios. Our findings provide a systematic landscape of current MLLM counting capabilities and offer a roadmap for developing more grounded and reliable multimodal systems. The dataset is available at https://mm-mvr.github.io/HoloCount/.

</details>


### [105] [MASS: Multiplayer World Models with Authoritative Shared State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06257)
*Ziqi Cai,Siqi Yang,Yimu Wang,Zixian Gao,Yunheng Liu,Shuchen Weng,Erwin Wu,Kaipeng Zhang,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MAS（Multiplayer world models with Authoritative Shared State），通过显式解耦世界动态与视图渲染，将逻辑引擎与渲染引擎分离，实现大规模多人环境下的可扩展且视图一致的视频世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型在多人环境中存在根本性缺陷：世界状态与视图相关视觉潜在表示纠缠在一起，导致冗余计算、跨视图不一致和可扩展性差。

Method: 受多人游戏架构启发，MAS包含两个解耦组件：(1) 学习型Logic Engine——无需手工编写转移函数，从联合动作推进全局权威类型化状态，作为唯一的循环记忆和同步参考；(2) 学习型Rendering Engine——根据共享状态按需为任意相机生成独立且一致的视图。

Result: 在多人Snake基准上，MAS在状态准确性和跨视图一致性方面优于最先进的多视图基线；能够以1,024个并发玩家推进预测世界10,000个循环步骤。

Conclusion: 显式的权威状态建模为可扩展、一致的多智能体世界模拟提供了实用基础，表明解耦状态逻辑与视图渲染是解决多人世界模型扩展性问题的有效方向。

Abstract: Current video world models struggle in multiplayer environments because they entangle world state with view-dependent visual latents, leading to redundant compute, view inconsistencies, and poor scalability. We propose MAS (Multiplayer world models with Authoritative Shared State) to resolve this limitation. Inspired by multiplayer game architectures, MAS disentangles world dynamics and view rendering. A learned Logic Engine advances a global, authoritative typed state from joint actions without any hand-written transition function, acting as the sole recurrent memory and synchronization reference. From this shared state, a learned Rendering Engine generates independent and consistent views for any requested camera on demand. This explicit disentangling allows MAS to achieve superior state accuracy and lower cross-view inconsistency compared to state-of-the-art multi-view baselines on a matched multiplayer Snake benchmark. It advances predicted worlds with 1,024 concurrent players for 10,000 recurrent steps. Our results show that explicit, authoritative state modeling provides a practical foundation for scalable and consistent multi-agent world simulation.

</details>


### [106] [OTLesMix: Wasserstein Barycenter and Optimal Transport Map for Synthetic Lesion Generation with Diverse Shapes and Locations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06264)
*Robin Trombetta,Carole Lartizien*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 OTLesMix 的新型医学图像合成方法，利用 Wasserstein 重心和最优传输计划生成真实且多样的训练样本，在三个脑部病灶分割任务上将 Dice 分数提升了 2.9 至 6.6 个点，并优于现有的混合类数据增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于混合策略的数据增强方法（如简单的空间变换、强度修改或合成技术）虽然能生成新样本，但主要缺陷是生成病灶的形状和位置变异性有限，限制了模型泛化能力的提升。

Method: 提出 OTLesMix 方法，利用 Wasserstein 重心和最优传输计划来生成真实且多样化的合成样本，通过将不同真实样本进行最优传输混合，从而产生比传统混合方法更多样化的病灶形态和位置。

Result: 在三个脑部病灶分割任务上进行评估，与不使用合成数据的模型相比，Dice 分数提升 2.9 至 6.6 个点；同时优于基于混合的现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: OTLesMix 通过最优传输理论有效解决了现有混合数据增强方法中病灶形状和位置变异有限的问题，能够生成更真实多样的合成样本，显著提升医学图像分割性能。

Abstract: The development of deep learning over the past decade has revolutionized medical imaging segmentation, allowing the extraction of precise descriptors from large volumes to characterize pathologies. Data augmentation is a technique widely regarded as a way to improve model training. It includes simple transformations like spatial operations or intensity modifications, but also more advanced synthesis techniques. Their goal is to generate new realistic samples from an existing dataset to diversify the images used during training. Among them, several propose different mixing strategies to combine real samples. However, one of their major shortcomings is to yield limited variability in terms of generated lesion shapes and locations. In this work, we introduce a novel image synthesis method, called OTLesMix, that leverages Wasserstein barycenter and optimal transport plan to generate realistic and diverse samples. We evaluated our method on three brain lesion segmentation tasks, on which it improves the Dice score compared to a model trained without synthetic data by 2.9 to 6.6 points, and outperforms state-of-the-art mix-based methods.

</details>


### [107] [TLNM: Externally Validated Tooth Detection, Numbering and Segmentation from Smartphone Photographs Using Mask R-CNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06275)
*Arash Nedaei,Henna Tiensuu,Elina Väyrynen,Saujanya Karki,Jaakko Suutala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于智能手机照片的牙齿定位与编号模型，采用定制化的Mask R-CNN管道，通过掩膜灰世界白平衡算法和解剖约束检测层处理患者生成数据的变异性，在内部和外部测试集上均取得了优异性能，并作为开源容器化API发布，为远程牙科筛查提供了可扩展的低成本基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球数十亿人存在口腔健康问题，但专业牙科护理的成本和可及性限制了预防性口腔保健。现有研究依赖临床级X光片或口腔内窥镜图像，公众无法用于自我筛查，亟需一种基于普通智能手机照片的自动化牙齿分析方案。

Method: 开发了一个定制化的Mask R-CNN管道，训练于1,272张标注的智能手机图像。管道包含两个领域自适应机制：(1) 掩膜灰世界白平衡算法用于校正人工色彩偏差；(2) 解剖约束检测层用于强制结构有效性并抑制假阳性。评估分为四个阶段：内部留存测试、独立外部测试、描述性消融研究和基于折叠的训练稳定性分析。

Result: 内部测试集上，模型达到实例掩膜AP@50为0.818，类别感知PQ为0.780，操作F1为0.884。训练稳定性良好，十次运行中实例掩膜AP@50标准差仅为0.009。外部数据集上，尽管人口、传感器和采集协议存在差异，模型仍达到实例掩膜AP@50为0.901，类别感知PQ为0.832，操作F1为0.928。

Conclusion: 消费级智能手机图像能够支持自动化的牙齿级解剖映射，为资源受限环境中远程筛查和远程牙科提供了可扩展、潜在低成本的基础。推理管道已作为开源容器化API发布。

Abstract: Oral health issues affect billions globally, but the cost and limited access to professional dental care hinder preventive oral healthcare. Research relies on clinical-grade radiographs or intraoral camera images, unavailable for public self-screening. This study introduces a tooth localisation and numbering model for smartphone photographs. We developed a customised Mask Region-based Convolutional Neural Network (Mask R-CNN) pipeline trained on 1,272 annotated smartphone images. To address variability in patient-generated health data, the pipeline incorporates two domain-informed mechanisms: a masked gray-world white-balancing algorithm to mitigate artificial colour casts and an anatomically constrained detection layer to enforce structural validity and suppress false positives. Evaluation comprised four stages: internal held-out testing, independent external testing, a descriptive ablation study, and fold-based training stability analysis using the same internal test set. On the internal test set, the model achieved an instance-mask AP@50 of 0.818, class-aware PQ of 0.780, and operational F1 of 0.884. Training stability showed limited between-model variation: across ten runs, instance-mask AP@50 had a standard deviation of 0.009. On the external dataset, the model achieved an instance-mask AP@50 of 0.901, class-aware PQ of 0.832, and operational F1 of 0.928 despite differences in population, sensors, and acquisition protocols. The inference pipeline is available as an open-source, containerised API. These results demonstrate that consumer-grade smartphone imagery can support automated tooth-level anatomical mapping, offering a scalable, potentially low-cost foundation for remote screening and tele-dentistry in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [108] [UQ-Loc: Uncertainty-Aware LiDAR Scene Coordinate Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06307)
*Jacek Komorowski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UQ-Loc扩展了LightLoc架构，通过各向异性高斯协方差头预测每个体素的3x3正定协方差矩阵，实现对LiDAR场景坐标回归中偶然不确定性的建模，从而提升6-DoF定位精度并提供良好校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR场景坐标回归方法仅产生确定性预测，忽略了偶然不确定性（aleatoric uncertainty），这限制了系统的鲁棒性和下游决策能力。作者旨在通过显式建模不确定性来改进定位精度和可靠性。

Method: 在LightLoc架构上增加各向异性高斯协方差头，预测每个体素的完整3x3正定协方差矩阵；训练时使用NLL损失并结合kNN空间平滑正则化；推理时采用改进的SC2-PCR求解器，包括不确定性加权的种子评分和马氏距离内点检验；并使用ECE作为不确定性质量评估指标。

Result: 实验表明UQ-Loc在6-DoF定位精度上取得了一致的改进，同时生成了校准良好的协方差矩阵。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模偶然不确定性并提供良好的校准，UQ-Loc在提升定位精度的同时为下游决策提供了可靠的不确定性信息，验证了在LiDAR SCR任务中引入不确定性估计的有效性。

Abstract: LiDAR-based Scene Coordinate Regression (SCR) maps point clouds directly to 3D scene coordinates, enabling precise 6-DoF localisation without explicit map retrieval. However, existing methods produce deterministic predictions, discarding aleatoric uncertainty that could improve robustness and downstream decision-making. We present UQ-Loc, which extends the LightLoc architecture with an anisotropic Gaussian covariance head that predicts a full 3x3 positive-definite covariance matrix per voxel. Training uses a Negative Log-Likelihood (NLL) loss augmented with a kNN-based spatial smoothness regulariser, while inference employs a modified SC2-PCR solver with uncertainty-weighted seed scoring and a Mahalanobis-distance inlier test. We adopt Expected Calibration Error (ECE) as a principled metric for evaluating the quality of the predicted uncertainty. Experiments demonstrate that UQ-Loc achieves consistent improvement in 6-DoF localization accuracy while producing well-calibrated covariances.

</details>


### [109] [Does FLAIR super-resolution erase or hallucinate small white-matter lesions?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06311)
*Zahra Khodakarami,Yue Li,Pulkit Khandelwal,John Detre,Sandhitsu Das,Christopher Brown,David Wolk,Paul Yushkevich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了将超分辨率重建（SR）应用于FLAIR图像后再进行白质高信号（WMH）分割是否会造成病变信息丢失。通过在29例ADNI高分辨率FLAIR数据上模拟3mm和5mm厚层采集，比较了多对比隐式神经表征（INR）、单对比自监督模型（ECLARE）和三次插值三种重建方法，发现SR的主要影响是消除小的真实病变而非产生幻觉病变，且随层厚增加而加剧；ECLARE在保留小病变信号上表现最佳，INR并不优于三次插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FLAIR图像在临床中通常以厚层采集，导致面内分辨率差。虽然超分辨率被广泛用于恢复各向同性体积，但尚未有研究考察在WMH分割前应用SR是否会擦除真实小病变或产生幻觉病变，这一不确定性限制了SR在WMH分析中的应用。

Method: 使用ADNI队列中29例1mm各向同性高分辨率FLAIR扫描，由专家手动分割WMH。将高分辨率图像降质为模拟3mm和5mm厚层采集，分别用多对比INR、单对比自监督模型ECLARE和三次插值上采样到高分辨率网格。在四种WMH分割方法中选择对HR小病变最敏感的MARS-WMH进行分析，评估指标包括检测灵敏度、擦除率（HR检测到但重建后丢失的病变）和幻觉率（手动和HR分割中均不存在的预测成分），以原始厚层和HR FLAIR分别作为下界和上界基线。

Result: SR的主要效应是擦除小的真实病变而非产生幻觉，且擦除率随切片厚度增加而升高。尽管所有重建方法都优于原始厚层的病变检测，但ECLARE在两种厚度下均最好地保留小病变信号，而INR的性能不优于三次插值。

Conclusion: 超分辨率重建在WMH分割前的应用需要谨慎选择方法，因为不同SR算法对小病变信号的保留能力差异显著。ECLARE在该任务上表现最优，而INR未见明显优势；临床应用中需权衡SR带来的检测增益与真实小病变被擦除的风险。

Abstract: White matter hyperintensities (WMH), bright regions on Fluid-attenuated Inversion Recovery (FLAIR) scans are associated with cerebrovascular pathology and neurodegeneration. FLAIR is usually acquired with thick slices in clinical settings, giving it poor through-plane resolution. Super-resolution (SR) is a widely used method for recovering an isotropic volume from an anisotropic scan. Yet whether applying it prior to WMH segmentation preserves lesion content remains unknown: a model may erase small real lesions or hallucinate absent ones. We used 1-mm isotropic high-resolution (HR) FLAIR scans from 29 individuals in the ADNI cohort, each manually segmented for WMH by an expert. Then, we degraded each to simulated 3 and 5 mm through-plane acquisitions. Multi-contrast implicit neural representation (INR), a single-contrast self-supervised model (ECLARE), and cubic interpolation were used to upsample them onto the HR grid. WMH segmentation from a simulated thick slice and the original HR FLAIR set the floor and ceiling, respectively, for the per-lesion analysis. Of four WMH segmentation methods (WMH-SynthSeg, segcsvd, MARS-WMH, TrUE-Net), we ran the analysis under the most sensitive one to small lesions on HR (MARS-WMH) with the evaluation metrics of detection sensitivity, erasure rate (HR-detected lesions lost after reconstruction), and hallucination rate (predicted components absent from both the manual and HR segmentation). The dominant effect of SR was erasure of small real lesions, not hallucination, and it increased with slice thickness, though every reconstruction still improved lesion detection over the raw thick slice. ECLARE recovered small lesion signal best at both thicknesses, while the INR was no better than cubic interpolation.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MS-MLB是一个可复现的开源基准测试框架，用于基于全血RNA表达数据对多发性硬化症（MS）与健康对照进行分类，采用共享的无泄漏流水线评估多种机器学习算法，梯度提升在留出集上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多发性硬化的诊断依赖临床评估、影像学及实验室证据，过程复杂且可能延误。全血RNA表达数据可能包含疾病相关免疫信号，机器学习可用于辅助研究，但缺乏统一、可复现、无泄漏的评估基准来公平比较不同算法在该任务上的表现。

Method: 利用公开的GSE17048队列，构建MS与健康对照的二分类任务。设计共享且防数据泄漏的流水线，包含嵌套交叉验证、独立的未触碰留出集、Bootstrap置信区间、ROC与PR分析、校准度测量，并引入探索性的MS Research Score指标。框架内置外部模型提交通路。

Result: 在留出集上，梯度提升算法在MS Research Score上排名第一，得分为93.83，AUC-ROC为0.989，灵敏度为0.950，特异度为0.778，F1分数为0.927，Brier分数为0.050。

Conclusion: 作者强调MS-MLB是首个针对GSE17048全血RNA数据中MS与健康对照分类的开放基准，内置文档化的外部模型提交通路。但该评分仅用于研究对比，未经临床验证，不能替代临床诊断，且其贡献范围较窄，局限于这一特定任务与数据集。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [111] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFTER框架，通过挖掘冻结预测模型残差中的可解释特征来驱动轻量级后置校正器，在六个公共数据集和六个冻结骨干上超越所有专用特征工程系统，将最弱骨干的误差降低最多27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测模型往往以结构性、可重复的方式失败，而通过微调修复这些失败成本高昂。本文旨在提出一种无需微调主干模型、仅通过挖掘模型残差中的可解释特征来驱动轻量级后置校正的方法。

Method: CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning) 框架包含两个互补的特征生成器：(1) 对原始输入通道进行组合搜索的合成方法；(2) 利用大语言模型提出命名特征组合、二元标志和短可执行代码。所有候选特征无论来源如何，都通过单一的基于验证集的门控机制接受或拒绝，最后由验证集选择的校正器应用被接受的特征或保持预测不变。这种源无关的管线还允许在相同条件下评估先前的特征工程系统，从而将预测改进归因于特征来源本身。

Result: 在六个公共数据集和六个冻结骨干模型上，CRAFTER在每个特征预算下都超越了所有专用特征工程系统，大约是仅使用校正器本身所获改进的两倍，并将最弱骨干的误差降低最多27%。这些改进对不同LLM后端具有鲁棒性，且在微调后的骨干上同样有效。

Conclusion: 校正性特征发现（建模模型失败过程而非数据生成过程）是一种有效且经济的预测改善策略；CRAFTER通过源无关的验证门控机制将多种特征生成器统一起来，不仅实现了最佳性能，还提供了一个可用于严格归因和公平比较的工具。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [112] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于编写基于大语言模型（LLM）流程的概率编程语言，使开发者能够量化并传播整个应用流程中的不确定性，同时无需修改流程逻辑即可实验不同的推理缩放技术。论文通过实验研究和针对Rocq定理证明器的案例分析展示了该能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM输出缺乏准确性和置信度度量，在包含多次LLM调用及其他工具的流程中，不确定性会被累积放大，导致开发者和终端用户难以信任最终结果。现有方法难以在保持流程逻辑不变的前提下灵活地量化和传播不确定性。

Method: 提出一种概率编程语言，用于编程基于LLM的流程。该语言允许开发者量化并传播整个应用流程中的不确定性，并支持实验不同的推理缩放技术（如多次采样、自我一致性等），且无需在流程逻辑之外添加任何额外代码。

Result: 通过实验研究证明了该语言在量化和传播不确定性方面的能力，并通过构建一个用于Rocq定理证明器的代理作为案例研究，验证了该方法在实际复杂应用中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该概率语言为LLM应用开发提供了一种系统化的不确定性管理方案，在不干扰流程逻辑的前提下实现了灵活的推理缩放实验，有助于提升LLM应用的可信度和可靠性。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [113] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CGTime，一个4B参数的计算接地的时间序列-语言模型。它通过解耦感知与描述来解决多模态时间序列对齐中的"自监督陷阱"：用确定性代码从真实多变量时间序列中计算统计数据，再由LLM对这些预计算事实进行语言化表达。CGTime在多变量理解任务上超越了GPT-4o-mini和GPT-5.4-nano等更大规模的通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练多模态模型对齐时间序列与语言的方法存在两个根本问题：(1) 自监督陷阱——LLM读序列写描述，标签质量受限于模型自身的感知能力，数据永远无法教给模型超出标注者已知的内容；(2) 大多数数据集只用单变量，而真正重要的模式（跨通道相关性、领先-滞后结构、共现异常）都出现在多变量场景中，恰好是LLM感知能力最薄弱的地方。这两个问题形成了"可靠、真实、可扩展"的三角困境——现有方法最多只能满足其中两项。

Method: CGTime采用"感知与描述解耦"的设计：用确定性代码从真实开源多变量时间序列中计算一组统计量，然后由LLM将这些预计算的事实转化为自然语言描述。感知任务（LLM不擅长的）交由计算处理，LLM只负责表达（其擅长领域）。模型规模为4B参数。

Result: 在保持的多变量理解基准测试中，CGTime取得了最佳多变量事实分数（0.283），显著优于GPT-4o-mini（0.173）和GPT-5.4-nano（0.203），该差距通过了针对所有基线的Holm校正配对显著性检验。在生成字幕中陈述可验证数值事实的准确性更高，覆盖的统计属性范围也更广。

Conclusion: 将感知与描述解耦——计算负责统计、LLM负责表达——是解决时间序列与语言对齐中自监督局限的有效范式，使得一个小型专用模型能够在多变量理解任务上超越更大规模的通用模型。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [114] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将3D感知与空间推理显式解耦的范式，通过现成感知模型将物理世界重构为结构化3D证据，再用LoRA微调的LLM仅基于显式几何证据进行推理（DiSR框架），在无需大规模3D VQA训练的情况下达到竞争性能，同时兼具可解释性、模块化和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端空间推理模型依赖大规模训练联合隐式获取3D感知与推理能力，成本高昂且难以解释。作者观察到现代感知模型擅长估计连续3D几何，而LLM擅长组合与符号推理，两者优势互补，因此提出将感知与推理解耦的替代范式。

Method: DiSR框架包含两个阶段：1）使用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据（显式几何表示）；2）通过LoRA微调一个LLM，使其仅基于该显式几何证据进行空间推理，无需复杂工具调用策略或大规模3D VQA训练。

Result: 在主流空间推理基准上取得了与端到端方法相当的竞争性性能，同时提供了更好的可解释性、模块化设计和计算效率。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是空间智能中一种可扩展且有效的替代范式，相对于端到端建模具有显著优势，为空间推理研究提供了新的方向。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [115] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出因子化生成模型中常用的"将风格潜在变量z_s的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验即代表与类别信息独立"这一解释是错误的。作者证明了仅匹配边缘分布无法约束类别条件分布，风格潜在变量仍可高度预测标签。通过精确分解，作者展示了消除这一不匹配是因子化采样的四个必要条件之一但非充分条件。实验表明多种模型在全局MMD接近零的情况下，线性探针仍能以74%-100%准确率（随机水平10%）恢复类别标签；四种缓解策略可将探针准确率降至21%-46%，事后条件先验和风格库方法分别在不同数据集上取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化生成模型广泛采用将风格潜在变量z_s的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验的做法，并将此作为风格表示与类别信息独立的证据。作者认为这种解释是错误的：边缘分布匹配不约束类别条件分布，导致模型声称的因子化属性并未真正实现。该问题影响了模型的可解释性和可控生成能力，因此需要严格的理论分析和大规模实证验证。

Method: 作者首先推导了精确分解，证明边缘分布匹配是因子化采样所需的四个条件之一但非充分条件。随后使用一个案例研究模型和四种代表性潜在变量基线进行实证验证：测量全局MMD（最大均值差异）评估边缘匹配程度，通过线性探针恢复类别标签评估信息泄漏，并在六种独立扰动（模型容量、课程学习、先验几何、监督信号等）下测试泄漏的鲁棒性。最后测试了四种缓解策略及事后条件先验和风格库等方法的效果。

Result: 案例模型和四种基线在近零全局MMD的情况下，线性探针仍能以74%-100%准确率（随机水平10%）恢复类别标签；聚类准确率达到99.15%，但外部评估的类别条件生成仅16%成功。泄漏在六种扰动下持续存在。四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类别内依赖基本不变。事后条件先验在MNIST上可将外部生成评估提升至0.97（无需重训练），但在CIFAR-10上仅达0.41；经验风格库在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。

Conclusion: 任何仅基于风格潜在变量边缘分布计算的分歧度量都无法证明其与类别标签的独立性，仅报告边缘统计量不能验证因子化生成模型中通常声称的属性。这为因子化生成模型的评估和验证方法提出了重要警示，需要新的验证标准或更强的约束条件。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [116] [FlowAdam: Implicit Regularization via Geometry-Aware Soft Momentum Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06652)
*Devender Singh,Tarun Sheel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowAdam是一种混合优化器，将Adam与基于ODE的连续梯度流积分相结合。当EMA统计检测到困难地形时，FlowAdam切换到裁剪的ODE积分，并通过Soft Momentum Injection在模式转换中融合ODE速度与Adam动量，防止训练崩溃。在低秩矩阵/张量恢复、协同过滤等耦合参数优化任务上超越Adam、Lion和AdaBelief，同时在良好条件化任务（如CIFAR-10）上与Adam匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam等自适应矩方法使用基于梯度平方指数移动平均的对角逐坐标预条件器，这种对角缩放依赖于坐标系，难以处理密集或旋转的参数耦合问题（如矩阵分解、张量分解、图神经网络），因为每个参数被独立处理。这限制了Adam在耦合优化问题上的表现。

Method: 提出FlowAdam混合优化器：1）以Adam为基础优化器；2）当EMA统计量检测到地形困难时，切换到带裁剪的ODE积分进行连续梯度流优化；3）核心创新是Soft Momentum Injection，在模式转换期间将ODE速度与Adam动量软融合，取代了天真的硬切换方法，从而防止训练崩溃。

Result: 在低秩矩阵/张量恢复上降低留出误差10-22%，在Jester真实协同过滤数据集上降低6%，超过调优后的Lion和AdaBelief；在良好条件化的CIFAR-10任务上与Adam持平。MovieLens-100K验证了收益源于耦合参数交互而非偏置估计。消融研究表明soft injection至关重要：硬替换将准确率从100%降至82.5%。

Conclusion: FlowAdam通过引入连续梯度流ODE集成和Soft Momentum Injection，成功解决了Adam在耦合参数优化问题上的局限性，在不牺牲良好条件化任务性能的前提下，显著提升了困难（耦合）优化问题的泛化能力。该工作表明混合离散-连续优化范式是提升深度学习优化器鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Adaptive moment methods such as Adam use a diagonal, coordinate-wise preconditioner based on exponential moving averages of squared gradients. This diagonal scaling is coordinate-system dependent and can struggle with dense or rotated parameter couplings, including those in matrix factorization, tensor decomposition, and graph neural networks, because it treats each parameter independently. We introduce FlowAdam, a hybrid optimizer that augments Adam with continuous gradient-flow integration via an ordinary differential equation (ODE). When EMA-based statistics detect landscape difficulty, FlowAdam switches to clipped ODE integration. Our central contribution is Soft Momentum Injection, which blends ODE velocity with Adam's momentum during mode transitions. This prevents the training collapse observed with naive hybrid approaches. Across coupled optimization benchmarks, the ODE integration provides implicit regularization, reducing held-out error by 10-22% on low-rank matrix/tensor recovery and 6% on Jester (real-world collaborative filtering), also surpassing tuned Lion and AdaBelief, while matching Adam on well-conditioned workloads (CIFAR-10). MovieLens-100K confirms benefits arise specifically from coupled parameter interactions rather than bias estimation. Ablation studies show that soft injection is essential, as hard replacement reduces accuracy from 100% to 82.5%.

</details>


### [117] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架，通过四阶段数据合成为多模态大模型生成"评分标准理解"训练数据，将模型从"被评分的生成器"转变为"按评分标准执行的执行器"。仅用10K合成样本，将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，且增益可迁移到其他四种开源MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实多模态指令常包含多个优先级不同的要求，但现有多模态训练数据大多将指令遵循简化为回答单一自包含问题，模型缺乏对多规则、带优先级指令的理解和执行能力。

Method: 提出PRISM四阶段数据合成框架：(1) persona-task对生成；(2) 前缀引导的规则集生成；(3) 质量过滤的评分标准构建；(4) 结构化验证轨迹生成。同时提出PRISM-Eval评估基准，使用Loose和Strict两类指标，通过确定性匹配固定标签进行评估，无需推理时judge模型。

Result: 仅用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B在PRISM-Eval上的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时保持通用基准的平均性能；增益可迁移至四种额外开源MLLM（涵盖密集与MoE架构）。

Conclusion: 结构化评分标准监督是通往多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令遵循的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [118] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示优化器在因子化模型 W=UV^T 上的隐式低秩偏向取决于其是否满足规范等变性（gauge-equivariance）。梯度下降、动量、共享标量Adam、Muon和Shampoo满足该条件，而Adam、RMSProp等坐标级方法不满足，导致其丢失低秩恢复能力。作者提出结构定理刻画无记忆等变规则等价于Gram决定左预条件器，并通过传递定理将梯度流的路径性质推广到公共标量流，最终在矩阵感知、Transformer和超光谱数据上验证了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降在因子化模型上隐式偏向低秩解，但Adam从相同的小初始化出发却不具备此偏向。理解这一差异的根源不仅有助于解释优化器的行为差异，还能指导在特定任务中选择合适的优化器，因为基选择（basis choice）实质上决定了优化器选择哪个插值器（interpolant）。

Method: 作者将低秩偏向的差异追溯到损失函数的规范对称性（gauge symmetry），即 (U,V)↦(UQ,VQ) 的不变性。他们证明梯度流的低秩机制仅对规范等变（gauge-equivariant）的优化器可用，并给出两个核心定理：(1) 结构定理——记忆less等变规则恰好等同于Gram决定的左预条件器；(2) 传递定理——梯度流的路径性质可推广到公共标量流。此外，通过构造从坐标级到共享标量的单参数族预条件器来恢复偏向，并设计"频谱调度"（spectral schedule）来调和关于Muon的相互矛盾的报告。

Result: 在欠定矩阵感知上评估了九种更新规则：单参数族从坐标级到共享标量预条件器单调恢复低秩偏向，证实各向异性（anisotropy）是Adam等丢失偏向的原因。对Muon，等速率更新能精确恢复低秩目标，但随着谱尾增长优势消失。在Transformer中，Adam在第一步就分离两个规范等价的初始化，而等变优化器保持浮点精度，最终逐头不变量 W_Q^TW_K 的相对Frobenius距离相差56%。在两个超光谱数据集上，匹配训练损失时，梯度下降在最低采样密度下将留出错误降低43-44%，且在更低的有效秩下实现。

Conclusion: 基选择（basis choice）并非调参细节，而是决定优化器选择哪个插值器的关键决策。规范等变性是低秩恢复能力的必要条件（尽管不充分），这一理论框架为理解和设计具备隐式正则化能力的优化器提供了统一的视角。

Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

</details>


### [119] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuroSFT，一个参数高效的参数高效微调框架，将多任务监督微调（msft）中感知过拟合的调度状态从全模型检查点重构为紧凑可合并的适配器状态，冻结预训练骨干、仅训练注入适配器，在任务峰值处回滚适配器检查点并继续在剩余激活混合上训练。AuroSFT在五个骨干网络上平均准确率达61.36%，优于msft参考行的59.85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务SFT通常将异构数据混合视为单一优化问题，但不同任务在不同时间达到最佳泛化。msft通过任务级滚动、排除和回滚暴露了这种不匹配，但其原始公式将调度器状态物化为全模型检查点，导致阶段转换的存储、恢复和部署成本高昂。

Method: AuroSFT冻结预训练骨干，仅训练注入的适配器，在任务级峰值处回滚适配器检查点，并继续在剩余激活混合上训练。在层级别，每个适配器将AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层应用于低秩权重因子而非样本表示，从而更新保持输入线性、秩有界，且可精确合并到冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留骨干比较协议下，AuroSFT平均准确率达61.36%，而msft参考行为59.85%；AuroSFT在全部五个骨干网络上均取得更高准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT通过将msft的全模型调度状态压缩为紧凑可合并的适配器状态，在保持性能提升的同时大幅降低存储、恢复和部署开销，实现了参数高效的多任务SFT。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuroOFT方法，在量化正交微调（QOFT）基础上，为每个适配的线性层附加一个零起点、门控的低秩非线性残差分支，以突破QOFT仅能进行线性正交变换的限制。AuroOFT将激活映射到RMS归一化的紧凑潜空间，使用自适应非线性基与有界或令牌相关的门控机制。在初始状态时与QOFT功能等价，正交性仅作为分支级稳定属性而非组合非线性层的属性。在1.5B/3B Qwen2.5上，AuroOFT相比QOFT提升Macro-6达1.30-2.70%，相比QLoRA提升6.52-10.62%，并节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化正交微调（QOFT）虽然实现了低比特语言模型的参数高效适配，但其任务特定更新被限制在线性正交变换范围内，无法进行依赖输入的（input-dependent）非线性修正，这限制了模型的表达能力。因此需要在不牺牲量化兼容性和稳定性的前提下，引入输入相关的非线性校正能力。

Method: AuroOFT保留QOFT作为稳定的量化兼容分支，同时为每个适配的线性层附加一个零起始（zero-start）门控的低秩非线性残差分支。具体而言，激活信号被映射到RMS归一化的紧凑潜空间，使用自适应非线性基以及有界或令牌相关的门控机制。零初始化的上投影使AuroOFT在初始化时与QOFT功能上完全一致，而正交性仅作为分支级的稳定性属性，而非组合后非线性层的属性。

Result: 在匹配的数据、优化、解码和解析协议下，AuroOFT在1.5B/3B Qwen2.5设置上相比匹配的QOFT提升Macro-6达1.30-2.70%，超过QLoRA 6.52-10.62%，并在代表性规模上相对于QLoRA节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。小规模考试型多项选择数学集仅作为协议敏感性诊断使用。

Conclusion: AuroOFT通过结合QOFT的量化兼容正交分支与零起点门控的低秩非线性残差，成功在保持初始化功能等价性和量化稳定性的同时，提升了低比特语言模型的适配性能，并以更少的可训练参数超越了现有基线方法。代码已公开在匿名仓库中。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [121] [Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotopes for Identifiable and Refinable Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05454)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope（HProbZ），一种新型概率预测头，将不确定性分解为离散模态选择、有界系统性漂移和不可约随机噪声三类来源，分别以二值、有界和随机生成器表示，并支持闭式似然、单次前向传递的跨步精细化推理、可辨识性保证及解析风险与多模态共形集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概率预测头要么输出高斯混合，要么输出单一共形区域，无法区分真实预测任务中常常并存的不同不确定度来源：离散模态间的选择、选定模态内有界系统性漂移，以及不可约的随机噪声。

Method: 将三类不确定度分别建模为zonotope的二值生成器（binary generator）、有界生成器（bounded generator）和随机生成器（stochastic generator），通过卷积获得闭式似然。有界生成器在预测步间共享，使观察一步可代数性地细化其余所有步的预测分布（单次前向传播）。在推理阶段，利用共享结构提供解析的逐模态风险与分布无关的多模态共形集。

Result: 证明了三类生成器可从似然中可辨识（至多置换意义下），且HProbZ密度与任意有限高斯混合在表示上存在本质区别。在代表性预测基准上的实证分析表明，相比同编码器混合基线，该设计在有效性的同时提供了混合模型或凸共形预测器无法同时具备的结构性质。

Conclusion: HProbZ通过zonotope结构统一了离散、有界和随机三类不确定度的建模，兼具闭式似然、可辨识性保证、代数耦合的跨步推理和解析共形集等性质，是一种比混合模型或共形预测更具结构优势的概率预测方案。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction heads in neural networks typically output either a Gaussian mixture or a single conformal region. Neither separates the distinct sources of uncertainty often present in real prediction tasks: a discrete choice among modes, bounded systematic drift within the chosen mode, and irreducible stochastic noise. We introduce the Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ), an output head that represents these three sources as binary, bounded, and stochastic generators of a zonotope, and admits a closed-form likelihood by convolution. Sharing the bounded generator across prediction steps couples future predictions algebraically, so observing one step refines the predictive distribution at every remaining step in a single forward pass. We establish that the three generators are identifiable from the likelihood up to permutation, and that an HProbZ density is representationally distinct from any finite Gaussian mixture. The same shared structure provides analytic per-mode risk and distribution-free multi-modal conformal sets at inference time. Empirical analysis on representative prediction benchmarks supports the effectiveness of the design relative to same-encoder mixture baselines, while offering structural properties that mixture or convex-conformal predictors do not jointly provide.

</details>


### [122] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向散户投资者的、由FastAPI后端和Web仪表盘驱动的个性化税务感知投资管理应用。系统通过三阶段强化学习框架（自监督跨资产编码器、含意图路由器的MoE分配策略、LoRA轻量适配器）将自然语言投资目标映射到六个投资指令之一，并生成经券商（Alpaca纸交易）集成的实时投资组合建议。系统已完成端到端集成测试但尚未面向真实用户，回测结果属于部署前初步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散户投资者缺乏机构客户所享有的个性化、税务感知的投资组合管理能力。现有机器人投顾依赖静态规则化配置，而机构级系统又有账户门槛和技术栈限制，普通个人投资者无法使用。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 构建一个功能完整的应用：1）FastAPI后端+Web仪表盘；2）自然语言投资目标解析并路由至六种投资指令之一；3）三阶段强化学习系统：自监督跨资产编码器、带学习型意图路由器的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）分配策略、以及基于用户真实券商行为进行个性化推荐的LoRA适配器（无需重训共享模型）；4）包含多用户认证、预览-确认流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计操作完整性链；5）与Alpaca纸交易API端到端集成测试。

Result: 系统集成测试通过，14天walk-forward回测（含自举置信区间）作为部署前初步验证。同时报告了若干工程教训：静默失效的集成路径、第三方API挂起调用、以及端到端实证验证优于信任检查点元数据。

Conclusion: 系统处于部署前新兴应用阶段，功能完整并经集成测试，但尚未开放给真实用户。本文诚实地将其报告为预部署应用，并提供了通向完整部署的明确路径以及可泛化到其他基于外部实时数据源的RL系统的工程经验。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model. The system is functionally complete and integration-tested end-to-end against a live brokerage API (Alpaca, paper-trading mode), including multi-user authentication, a trust first preview-before-apply confirmation flow, daily email digests, and an auditable action-integrity chain, but has not yet been opened to real end-users; we report this honestly as an emerging, pre-deployment application with a concrete path to full deployment, alongside 14-day walk-forward backtests (bootstrapped confidence intervals included) as preliminary, pre-deployment validation rather than production performance. We also report several practical engineering lessons -- silently-inactive integration paths, hanging third-party API calls, and the value of end-to-end empirical verification over trusting checkpoint metadata -- that we believe generalize to other applied RL systems built on external, live data sources.

</details>


### [123] [Evaluating Machine Learning Models for Post-Wildfire Debris-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05265)
*Quinn Ledingham,Zhengsen Xu,Yimin Zhu,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Motasem Alkayid,Megan Greenwood,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了15种机器学习模型（包括TabPFN）用于预测野火后泥石流事件，通过重复分层交叉验证发现TabPFN性能最优（威胁评分0.637），SHAP分析揭示短时降雨强度是最重要的预测特征，并利用TabPFN生成合成数据增强模型性能，为泥石流预测提供了全面框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测野火后泥石流对减轻灾害至关重要，但现有机器学习模型面临三大挑战：泥石流与非泥石流事件在特征空间中重叠、模型可解释性需求、以及训练数据有限的问题。

Method: 使用美国西部流域尺度的野火后泥石流观测数据，比较15种模型（包括TabPFN）的性能；采用重复分层交叉验证评估；使用SHAP进行特征重要性分析；利用TabPFN生成合成样本进行数据增强并评估对各模型性能的影响。

Result: TabPFN在无增强条件下取得最高威胁评分0.637，与最优树模型接近；SHAP分析显示短时降雨强度和风暴累积量是最高排名的预测特征，而燃烧严重度和地形特征贡献较低；合成数据增强除CNN外提升了所有模型性能，其中深度学习模型平均威胁评分提升最大（+0.041）。

Conclusion: 本研究通过严格的模型基准测试、可解释的特征分析和合成数据增强，为改进野火后泥石流预测提供了全面框架，验证了TabPFN在此类小样本、特征重叠问题中的有效性。

Abstract: Prediction of post-wildfire debris flows is critical for mitigating hazards to communities, infrastructure, and resources during intense rainfall in recently burned areas. However, identifying reliable machine learning models is complicated by overlapping debris-flow and non-debris-flow events in feature space, the need for model interpretability, and limited training data. This paper addresses these challenges through a systematic evaluation of machine learning models in terms of predictive performance, feature importance, and synthetic data augmentation. Using basin-scale observations of post-wildfire debris-flow events across the western United States, we compare 15 models, including the Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN). Repeated stratified cross-validation shows that TabPFN achieves the highest unaugmented performance with a threat score of 0.637, closely followed by the best tree-based models. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) are used to identify the features driving predictions, revealing that short-duration rainfall intensity and storm accumulation consistently rank highest, while burn severity and terrain features contribute less. We further evaluate synthetic data augmentation using TabPFN-generated samples to address the scarcity of debris-flow observations. Synthetic augmentation improves the performance of all models except CNN, with the largest mean threat score increase of +0.041 among the deep learning models. By combining rigorous model benchmarking, interpretable feature analysis, and synthetic data augmentation, this work provides a comprehensive framework for improving post-wildfire debris-flow prediction.

</details>


### [124] [The Tamed Subgradient Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm beyond Convexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06283)
*Iosif Lytras,Nikolaos Makras,Sotirios Sabanis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对势函数同时具备非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸性的目标分布采样问题，提出了Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA)，一种直接基于次梯度的显式离散化算法，避免了昂贵的平滑步骤，并利用taming技术保证超线性情形的稳定性。作者推导了Wasserstein-2距离下的非渐近收敛界，且所有常数均显式追踪为维度和逆温度的函数，优于现有基于次梯度的Langevin算法的收敛率。此外，论文还给出了相关优化问题的超额风险估计，并以GPT-2系列LLM的正则化预训练势函数验证了假设，展示了SG-TULA的坐标提升变体在预训练中可与微调后的AdamW和Muon竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于次梯度的Langevin采样算法在处理同时具有非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸性的目标分布时，收敛速率欠佳，且常用方法依赖计算昂贵的平滑过程。作者旨在开发一种直接操作次梯度且无需平滑的显式算法，在非凸超线性非光滑的联合条件下提供更优的非渐近收敛保证，并验证其在大型语言模型预训练中的实际可行性。

Method: 提出SG-TULA算法：对Langevin扩散进行离散化，直接使用次梯度而非光滑梯度；引入taming技术（对次梯度进行归一化/截断）以应对超线性增长带来的不稳定性，构造稳定的显式格式。理论分析上，在Wasserstein-2距离下推导非渐近收敛界，显式追踪所有常数对维度与逆温度的依赖；同时给出优化视角的超额风险估计。实验上，针对GPT-2谱系LLM的正则化预训练势验证假设条件，并引入SG-TULA的坐标提升变体进行预训练，与微调AdamW和Muon对比。

Result: 在Wasserstein-2距离下获得了更优的非渐近收敛速率，优于以往基于次梯度的Langevin算法；提供了显式常数依赖的收敛保证以及相应的超额风险界。在实证层面，坐标提升的SG-TULA在GPT-2谱系LLM预训练中取得了与微调AdamW和Muon相当的性能，而后者目前缺乏可比的非渐近理论保证。

Conclusion: SG-TULA为同时具有非光滑、超线性梯度增长和非凸性的势函数提供了一种高效且理论上可保证的采样算法，兼具理论与实证优势：不仅在Wasserstein-2距离下获得改进的收敛速率，还在实际的LLM预训练任务中展现了竞争力，填补了优化方法（如AdamW、Muon）缺乏非渐近保证的空白。

Abstract: We study the problem of sampling from target distributions whose potentials are simultaneously non-smooth, subject to superlinear gradient growth, and non-convex. We introduce the Subgradient Tamed Unadjusted Langevin Algorithm (SG-TULA), a discretisation of the Langevin diffusion that operates directly on subgradients, without relying on computationally demanding smoothing procedures. To handle the superlinear regime, taming techniques are employed to produce a stable, explicit scheme. We derive non-asymptotic convergence bounds in Wasserstein-2 distance, with all constants tracked explicitly in terms of dimension and inverse temperature, improving upon the currently known rates for subgradient-based Langevin algorithms. We further provide excess risk estimates for the associated optimisation problem. We verify the assumptions, with explicit constants, for the regularized pretraining potential of a LLM in the GPT-2 lineage and the boosted coordinate-wise variant of SG-TULA pretrains the former competitively against finetuned AdamW and Muon, for which no comparable non-asymptotic guarantees are presently available.

</details>


### [125] [Rectifying Geometric Misalignment: Online Source-Free Adaptation for Class-Imbalanced EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05315)
*Shiwen Chu,Shanglin Li,Motoaki Kawanabe,Reinmar Kobler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSPDIM（在线SPD流形信息最大化），一种无源在线无监督域适应（UDA）框架，用于解决基于EEG的脑机接口（BCI）中由标签分布动态变化（label shift）引起的黎曼流形几何错位问题。OSPDIM通过在切空间映射中引入流形约束的偏置参数，并利用信息最大化进行在线优化，校正不平衡数据流导致的几何偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的黎曼对齐方法（如RCT）虽然能有效处理协变量偏移，但隐式假设类别先验均衡。在真实在线BCI场景中，标签分布动态变化会产生标签偏移，导致标准对齐技术在几何上错位目标数据分布，影响模型泛化性能。

Method: OSPDIM在切空间映射中引入了一个受流形约束的偏置参数，通过信息最大化（information maximization）目标在线优化该偏置参数，以校正由不平衡数据流引起的几何倾斜。与依赖全局批统计量的离线方法不同，OSPDIM能够实时估计并校正几何偏差。

Result: 在2D SPD矩阵上的仿真实验直观展示了OSPDIM成功纠正了标准居中对齐无法解决的错位问题。在多个运动想象数据集上的大量实验表明，OSPDIM在严重的类别不平衡在线适应场景下显著优于标准Riemannian基线方法。

Conclusion: OSPDIM为实际可插拔（plug-and-play）的BCI系统提供了鲁棒的在线域适应解决方案，有效解决了标签偏移背景下黎曼流形对齐失效的问题。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) based Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) often require unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) to generalize across subjects and sessions. While Riemannian alignment methods like the Riemannian Centering Transformation (RCT) are effective for handling covariate shifts, they implicitly assume balanced class priors. However, in realistic online BCI scenarios, the label distributions vary dynamically (label shift), causing standard alignment techniques to geometrically misalign the target data distributions. In this work, we propose OSPDIM (Online SPD manifold information maximization), a source-free online UDA framework designed to address label shifts on the Riemannian manifold. OSPDIM introduces a manifold-constrained bias parameter into the tangent space mapping, which is optimized via information maximization to correct the geometric skew caused by imbalanced data streams. Unlike offline methods relying on global batch statistics, OSPDIM estimates and corrects geometric bias on-the-fly. Simulations on 2D SPD matrices visually demonstrate that OSPDIM successfully rectifies the misalignment where standard centering fails. Extensive experiments on multiple motor imagery datasets show that OSPDIM significantly outperforms standard Riemannian baselines, particularly in challenging online adaptation scenarios with severe class imbalance, offering a robust solution for practical, plug-and-play BCI systems.

</details>


### [126] [Surv-IPTB: An Attention-Based Model for Estimating Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit with Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06288)
*Lev V. Utkin,Stanislav K. Kogan,Andrei V. Konstantinov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Surv-IPTB，一个基于注意力机制的框架，用于在生存分析中估计个体治疗获益概率（IPTB），将问题转化为二分类任务并通过区间值概率处理右删失数据，在多种非线性合成数据集上优于T-learner、S-learner等元学习方法基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生存分析情境下，个体治疗获益概率（IPTB）的准确估计对于个性化医疗决策至关重要，但现有元学习方法（如T-learner、S-learner）在处理非线性复杂特征空间和右删失数据时性能显著退化，缺乏一个可扩展且统计上严谨的解决方案。

Method: （1）将IPTB估计重新表述为二分类问题，通过对治疗组和对照组患者的两两比较实现；（2）通过不精确概率表示（区间值概率）原则性地处理右删失观测；（3）采用具有可学习查询-键变换的注意力机制，实现灵活、数据驱动的成对比较聚合，同时为删失情况学习软类别概率。

Result: 在具有复杂非线性结构（螺旋形、钟形、圆形特征空间）的合成数据集上，该模型在不同删失率和治疗效果强度下均保持稳健性能，始终优于配备随机生存森林、Cox比例风险模型和Beran估计器的T-learner与S-learner基线，尤其在传统方法显著退化的非线性场景中优势明显。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于注意力的框架为生存情境下的个性化治疗获益评估提供了可扩展且统计上严谨的解决方案，优于现有元学习基线，代码已公开。

Abstract: This work presents a novel attention-based framework for estimating the Individual Probability of Treatment Benefit (IPTB) in survival analysis contexts. The proposed model, called Surv-IPTB, directly quantifies the probability that a specific patient will experience extended survival time under treatment versus control. We reformulate IPTB estimation as a binary classification problem, leveraging pairwise patient comparisons across treatment and control cohorts. The framework incorporates a principled handling of right-censored observations through imprecise probability representations, where uncertain treatment effects are characterized by interval-valued probabilities. An attention mechanism with learnable query-key transformations enables flexible, data-driven aggregation of pairwise comparisons, while simultaneously learning soft class probabilities for censored cases. Through extensive experiments on synthetic datasets with complex nonlinear structures, including spiral, bell-shaped, and circular feature spaces, we demonstrate that our approach maintains robust performance across varying censoring rates and treatment effect strengths. The model consistently outperforms meta-learner baselines (T-learner and S-learner) equipped with random survival forests, Cox proportional hazards, and Beran estimators, particularly in challenging nonlinear scenarios where conventional methods exhibit significant degradation. The results establish the proposed attention-based framework as a scalable and statistically principled solution for personalized treatment benefit assessment in survival settings. The code implementing the model is publicly available.

</details>


### [127] [QEvict: Recoverable Quantized KV Eviction for Attention-Drift-Robust Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05326)
*Ayushman Garg,Akshita Gupta,Shaswata Bhattacharya,Abhishek Gupta,Sandeep Kumar,Manoj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QEvict，一种三层KV缓存管理方案，用可恢复的驱逐策略替代传统二元的保留或删除策略，通过量化中间层窗口并在其重新变重要时反量化回全精度，在固定内存预算下保留更广泛的历史上下文，同时为最重要的区域维持全精度。

相关结论均在论文分析基础上获得，未发现违规内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型推理日益受限于KV缓存的记忆体占用。现有主流工作基于注意力分数驱逐不重要的token，但这类策略隐含一个不可逆的假设：一旦token被驱逐，便永远不会再次变得有用。论文发现该假设在解码过程中是脆弱的——随着生成查询的演化，token和窗口的重要性会发生漂移，导致标准驱逐策略永久丢弃那些在全缓存模型下稍后会获得大量注意力的状态。

Method: 论文首先引入两个诊断指标：Future Missed Mass（衡量被丢弃状态未来获得的注意力）和Global LIR（衡量历史非活跃区域被重新激活的程度），用以刻画驱逐策略的失效行为。在此基础上提出QEvict三层KV缓存管理方案：第一层将高置信度窗口以全精度保留；第二层将中间窗口存储在量化可恢复层；第三层仅删除最低置信度窗口。解码过程中，累积注意力分数动态更新窗口重要性；当某个被量化的窗口重新变得重要时，将其反量化并提升至全精度层。

Result: 在长上下文理解、检索和推理基准上，QEvict相较于代表性的驱逐策略和量化基线始终表现更好，减少了被错过的注意力并改善了信息保留。

Conclusion: 传统KV缓存驱逐策略的不可逆删除假设在解码过程中并不成立；通过引入可恢复的量化中间层，可以在固定内存预算下权衡历史保真度与精确度，实现更优的推理性能。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model inference is increasingly constrained by the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache. A dominant line of work reduces this footprint by evicting tokens that appear unimportant under attention-derived scores. However, such policies make an implicit irreversible decision: once a token is evicted, it cannot become useful again. We show that this assumption is brittle during decoding. Token and window importance drift as generated queries evolve, causing standard eviction policies to permanently discard states that later receive substantial attention under the full-cache model. To characterize this behaviour, we introduce Future Missed Mass and Global LIR, two diagnostics that measure future attention assigned to discarded states and the reactivation of historically inactive regions. We propose QEvict, a three-tier KV-cache management scheme that replaces binary retain-or-delete eviction with recoverable eviction. QEvict maintains high-confidence windows in full precision, stores intermediate windows in a quantized recoverable tier, and deletes only the lowest-confidence windows. During decoding, cumulative attention scores update window importance and when a quantized window becomes important again, it is dequantized and promoted to the full-precision. Under a fixed memory budget, this design preserves broader historical context while retaining exact full precision for the most important regions. Across long-context understanding, retrieval, and reasoning benchmarks, QEvict consistently improves over representative eviction and quantization baselines, reducing missed attention and improving information retention

</details>


### [128] [DG-FedReuse: Proxy-Gradient-Gated Cached-Update Reuse with Matched Sparse Uplink Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05358)
*Rahil Aftab,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Soumya Mazumdar,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-FedReuse，一种在联邦学习模拟器层面利用客户端缓存的年龄衰减更新来减少上行通信的机制。通过随机头梯度差异代理与轮次相关阈值控制复用，并配合硬缓存年龄限制和最小新鲜客户端配额。在6个图像分类数据集、50个虚拟客户端、Dirichlet标签异质性(α=0.5)和3个种子的条件下，90轮预算内实现了83.36-85.42%的建模更新数据字段上行节省，相比Top-K FedAvg的76.88%有显著提升，但精度差异为-5.29至-0.14个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习反复进行本地优化和模型更新传输，通信开销成为主要瓶颈。现有方法未充分探索在模拟器层面利用客户端缓存的旧更新来减少通信成本，同时需要保证模型精度不显著下降。

Method: DG-FedReuse机制：(1)允许选中的客户端在随机头梯度差异代理低于轮次相关阈值时贡献年龄衰减的缓存更新；(2)设置硬缓存年龄限制和最小新鲜客户端配额来约束复用；(3)新鲜更新使用自适应逐张量Top-K数值字段表示。实验设置包括6个图像分类数据集、50个虚拟客户端、Dirichlet标签异质性(α=0.5)和3个种子。

Result: 在90轮预算下，DG-FedReuse实现83.36-85.42%的建模更新数据字段上行节省（Top-K FedAvg为76.88%）；种子对齐精度差异为-5.29至-0.14个百分点。考虑对称稠密模型下行敏感性后，主要节省降至41.68-42.71%，对Top-K FedAvg的增量收益降至3.24-4.27个百分点。最佳测试精度相对匹配FedAvg为-2.38至+0.45个百分点（仅作为探索性档案证据）。

Conclusion: DG-FedReuse在模拟器层面有效减少上行通信量，但因通信节省结论高度依赖计算边界（是否计入模型下行），需谨慎解读。作者明确承认该研究仅刻画模拟器中的复用规则，未建立无偏泛化、端到端带宽减少、运行时或能量节省、更快收敛等结论，也未证明优于现有过时更新和惰性聚合方法。

Abstract: Federated learning repeatedly incurs local optimization and model-update transmission. We study DG-FedReuse, a simulator-level mechanism that allows selected clients to contribute age-decayed cached updates when a stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy remains below a round-dependent threshold. A hard cache-age limit and minimum fresh-client quota constrain reuse, while fresh updates use an adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation. Experiments cover six image-classification datasets, 50 virtual clients, Dirichlet label heterogeneity (α=0.5), and three seeds. At a common 90-round budget, DG-FedReuse yields 83.36-85.42% modeled update-data-field uplink saving, compared with 76.88% for matched Top-K FedAvg; the seed-aligned accuracy differences range from -5.29 to -0.14 percentage points. Best-observed test accuracies obtained under test-controlled checkpointing are retained only as exploratory archival evidence and range from -2.38 to +0.45 percentage points relative to matched FedAvg. A symmetric dense-model-downlink sensitivity reduces the headline saving to 41.68-F42.71% and the incremental gain over Top-K FedAvg to 3.24-4.27 percentage points, demonstrating the dependence of communication conclusions on the accounting boundary. The study characterizes the proposed reuse rule in the implemented simulator; it does not establish unbiased generalization, end-to-end bandwidth reduction, runtime or energy savings, faster convergence, or superiority over existing stale-update and lazy-aggregation methods.

</details>


### [129] [Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs) for Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05371)
*Hailong Jiang,Emran Hossain,Feng Yu,Jianfeng Zhu,Guilin Zhang,Wulan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量子结构世界模型（QSWMs），一种受量子理论启发的预测性世界建模框架，采用结构化潜状态、潜转移算子和测量启发的解码映射，并通过三个基础性质及细胞自动机实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型（World Models）大多使用经典向量、概率分布、循环隐藏状态或Transformer激活来表示潜状态，而量子理论中的数学结构（如复值表示和密度矩阵）可能为世界建模提供有益的归纳偏置，但目前尚未被系统性探索。

Method: 提出QSWM框架，包括三类关键组件：结构化潜状态、潜转移算子以及测量启发的解码映射。建立三个理论性质：经典包含性（经典模型可被QSWM涵盖）、预测充分性（状态具有足够信息支持预测）、结构紧凑性（结构化表示效率）。随后实例化复值QSWM和密度矩阵类QSWM两种变体，在一维元胞自动机上进行评估，并与经典基线方法对比。

Result: 复值QSWM在局部预测方面展现出有潜力的性能表现，但密度矩阵类变体以及两者在长时程rollout预测方面均存在局限性。

Conclusion: 量子启发的结构化潜状态为世界建模提供了新的方向，复值表示展现出局部预测优势，但长时程预测和密度矩阵变体仍需进一步改进，为未来量子启发式世界模型的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: World models learn latent states that summarize interaction histories, evolve over time, and support prediction, simulation, or planning. Most existing world models represent these states using classical vectors, probability distributions, recurrent hidden states, or transformer activations. In this paper, we introduce Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs), a quantum-inspired framework for predictive world modeling with structured latent states, latent transition operators, and measurement-inspired decoding maps. We study whether mathematical structures inspired by quantum theory, such as complex-valued representations and density-matrix-like latents, provide useful inductive biases for world modeling. We establish three foundational properties: classical inclusion, predictive sufficiency, and structured compactness. We then instantiate complex-valued and density-matrix-like QSWM variants and evaluate them on elementary cellular automata against strong classical baselines. Results show promising local predictive potential for complex-valued QSWMs, while also revealing limitations in long-horizon rollout, density-matrix variants

</details>


### [130] [Spectral Distillation: From Nonlinear Dynamics to Linear State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05416)
*Liane Galanti,Devan Shah,Shlomo Fortgang,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从非线性动力系统观测中通过凸方法学习紧凑线性状态空间表示（LDS）的端到端可证明管线，包含两个阶段：先用观测谱滤波（OSF）学习隐式谱预测器，再通过谱到LDS蒸馏将其转化为显式循环线性动力系统。理论保证误差分解为指数小的蒸馏项和由Luenberger复杂度刻画的OSF学习项，且与潜在维度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接学习非线性动力系统的紧凑线性状态空间表示通常需要求解非凸的系统辨识问题，缺乏可证明的保证。作者希望避免直接求解非凸问题，转而通过凸学习方法获得具有理论保证的LDS表示。

Method: 两阶段管线：（1）观测谱滤波（OSF）：一种凸方法，学习隐式谱预测器，与系统的最优线性观测器竞争；（2）谱到LDS蒸馏：将隐式谱预测器转换为显式的循环线性动力系统。理论分析表明蒸馏后的LDS的平均预测误差可分解为指数小的蒸馏项和由最佳观测器的Luenberger复杂度控制的OSF学习项。

Result: 理论保证是维度无关的，只依赖观测器复杂度而非表示非线性系统所需的潜在维度。实验在线性LDS基准和MuJoCo行为克隆任务上表明，train-then-distill管线生成的紧凑LDS预测器匹配或优于直接训练基线。

Conclusion: 这是首个通过凸学习加可证明蒸馏从非线性动力学中提取最佳事后LDS表示的端到端可证明方法，兼具理论保证和实证表现。

Abstract: Can nonlinear dynamical systems be learned through a compact linear state-space representation, without directly solving a non-convex system-identification problem? We give a provable pipeline for doing so. Starting from observations of an unknown nonlinear dynamical system, we first learn an implicit spectral predictor using Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF), a convex method that competes with the best linear observer for the system. We then apply spectral-to-LDS distillation to convert this predictor into an explicit recurrent linear dynamical system. Our main theorem shows that the average prediction error of the distilled LDS decomposes into an exponentially-small distillation term and the OSF learning term governed by the Luenberger complexity of the best observer. The guarantee is dimension-free: it depends on observer complexity rather than on the latent dimension needed to represent the nonlinear system. To our knowledge, this yields the first end-to-end provable method for extracting a best-in-hindsight LDS representation of nonlinear dynamics through convex learning followed by provable distillation. Experiments on linear LDS benchmarks and MuJoCo behavior cloning show that the train-then-distill pipeline produces compact LDS predictors that match or outperform directly trained baselines.

</details>


### [131] [Perturbation Sensitivity at Convergence: A Simple Signal for Identifying Spuriously Correlated Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05419)
*Nilesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需组别标注的虚假相关性样本识别方法：在模型收敛后对输入施加固定扰动，观察到与虚假相关性一致的样本（受共享规则支配）预测更稳定，而不一致样本（受输入特定拟合支配）预测更脆弱。该方法只需两次前向传播，无需提前停止或验证集标注，在Waterbirds上将最差组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，接近使用真实组标签的85.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验风险最小化训练的模型在包含虚假相关性的数据上能达到高平均准确率，但在相关关系不成立的子群体上表现失败。现有识别受影响样本的方法依赖早期训练信号，需要利用带组标注的验证数据来选择干预的epoch超参数，这在实际场景中难以获得。

Method: 作者证明在模型收敛后（此时损失已无法区分两类群体），与虚假相关性一致的样本由共享规则分类，而其余样本通过输入特定的配置拟合，因此更加脆弱。对收敛模型的输入施加固定扰动，后者预测翻转的频率远高于前者。该方法只需对每个训练样本进行两次前向传播。

Result: 在Waterbirds数据集上，使用检测出的样本进行重平衡训练可将最差组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，相比之下使用真实组标签可获得85.8%。方法全程无需组标注，无需提前停止。

Conclusion: 收敛后的模型在输入扰动下的预测鲁棒性差异可作为有效的虚假相关样本识别信号，避免了早期训练方法对干预epoch选择和组标注验证集的依赖。

Abstract: Models trained by empirical risk minimization on data containing spurious correlations achieve high average accuracy while failing on subpopulations where the correlation does not hold. Existing methods for identifying the affected samples without group annotations rely on signals from early training, which requires locating the epoch at which to intervene, a hyperparameter typically selected using group-labeled validation data. We show that a usable signal is available after convergence, when loss no longer distinguishes the two populations. Samples consistent with the spurious correlation are classified by a shared rule, while the remaining samples are fit through configurations specific to individual inputs and are correspondingly more fragile. Applying a fixed perturbation to a converged model's inputs flips the predictions of the latter far more often than the former. The resulting procedure requires two forward passes per training sample, no group annotations at any stage, and no early-stopping epoch. Using the detected samples to rebalance training raises worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds from 57.3% to 80.8%, against 85.8% with ground-truth group labels.

</details>


### [132] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出信息流网络（IFNs），将生成流网络框架（AFlowNets）从不完全信息博弈扩展到不完全信息博弈，证明原有约束在不完全信息博弈中不适用于获得有效密度和训练目标，并提出广义化框架IFlowNets。初步实验表明，在三个标准博弈环境中，IFlowNets的性能和速度与OSMCCFR及标准RL方法相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然已有许多算法将强化学习（RL）与反事实遗憾（CFR）方法结合以权衡计算速度和性能，但在不完全信息博弈中生成式采样框架的研究却相对匮乏。作者希望将生成流网络框架扩展到不完全信息博弈，以探索其在博弈论应用中的潜力。

Method: 作者提出信息流网络（IFNs）框架，将Adversarial Flow Networks（AFlowNets）从不完全信息博弈推广到不完全信息博弈。首先证明了原有AFlowNets的约束在不完全信息博弈中无法获得有效的密度（对应玩家策略）和有效的训练目标；随后提出广义化版本IFlowNets以解决该问题，并证明其严格推广了AFlowNets。实验采用三个标准博弈环境进行验证。

Result: 在三个标准博弈环境的初步实验中，IFlowNets在性能和速度方面与Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret（OSMCCFR）以及标准RL方法相当或更优。同时，IFlowNets被证明是对AFlowNets的严格推广。

Conclusion: IFlowNets成功地将生成流网络扩展到不完全信息博弈场景，克服了原有框架的约束限制，并在实际博弈环境中展现了有竞争力的性能。这为不完全信息博弈中的生成式采样框架研究开辟了新的方向。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [133] [Why the Third Axis Is Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05423)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"freedom"（自由度）才是Explorative Modeling（XM）所谓的"第三条预训练轴"的本质，而非生成表现力。作者证明了XM损失与候选输出落入可接受区域的概率的K次幂相关，更大的K能提高匹配概率，从而增加自由度；并通过实验表明XM实际在优化自由度，且用自由度选择器在30次中29次优于验证集选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生成式训练中，单次输出比较会偏向产生单一常见答案的模型，而忽略保留更广行为库的模型。Explorative Modeling（XM）通过每次生成K个输出并对最接近的一个进行更新，宣称"探索"是继数据和计算之后的"第三条预训练轴"。作者旨在揭示XM真正优化的是什么，并将其与更普适的泛化理论（freedom）联系起来。

Method: 理论上，作者证明平均XM损失取决于候选输出错过可接受区域的概率，而探索使该概率变为K次方；并证明当K>1时，匹配概率随freedom增加。实证上，作者设计Forward XM实验（观察更大K是否提升freedom）、上下文依赖目标下的freedom测试，以及用只读未标注父语境（unlabelled parent contexts）的freedom选择器与验证集选择对比的实验。

Result: Forward XM实验中，更大的K提高了或饱和了实测freedom；在上下文依赖目标下，更大的K在每个测试值上都增加了freedom。在XM候选池对比中，freedom选择器在30个测试案例中赢得29个。此外，以往工作已证明freedom选择在归纳实验中以110-500%的优势击败MDL。

Conclusion: 生成表现力只是freedom的模式计数（mode-count）代理指标，丢弃了freedom泛化意义所依赖的延展结构（extension structure）。XM是一个手段，freedom才是目的；在分布偏移下选择freedom能显著改进XM效果。

Abstract: In generative training, a model produces an output and is penalised for its difference from an example. With one output per comparison, a model that produces one common answer can outperform a model retaining a broader repertoire. Explorative Modeling (XM) produces $K$ outputs per comparison and updates on the closest, claiming exploration as a "third pretraining axis" associated with generative expressivity. Here I show the third axis is actually freedom, meaning the weakness of the constraint implied by a model's behaviour. Previous work showed freedom is a property of function rather than form. Parameters, architecture, minimum-description-length (MDL), and data can vary while the behavioural constraint remains unchanged. It was formally proved that weakest models are likeliest to generalise, and freedom selection beat MDL by 110-500\% in induction experiments. I prove average XM loss depends on the chance a candidate misses an acceptable region, with exploration raising miss probability to power $K$. For $K>1$, match probability rises with freedom. I then demonstrate empirically that XM optimises for freedom. In a Forward XM experiment, larger $K$ increased or saturated measured freedom, and increased freedom at every tested value under context-dependent targets. I trained XM candidate pools and compared validation selection with a freedom selector that read unlabelled parent contexts. Freedom won in 29 of 30 cases. Generative expressivity is a mode-count proxy for freedom, that discards the extension structure that gives freedom its generalisation significance. XM is a means, freedom an end, and selecting for freedom improved XM under distribution shift.

</details>


### [134] [EvoHarness-RL: Learning Self-Evolving Runtime Harness for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05446)
*Xuying Ning,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Hanqing Zeng,Yuanchen Bei,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Li,Qifan Wang,Xiang Shen,Yifan Wu,Jiayi Liu,Hong Li,Yinglong Xia,Xiangjun Fan,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoHarness-RL框架，通过离线学习harness策略（状态形成与运行时控制），让长程LLM智能体在任务执行中自主构建和更新外部harness状态。在ALFWorld上使用Qwen3-8B达到96.9%成功率，并揭示了harness退火（训练内化harness使用模式）和harness演化（状态压缩与自适应）两种关键动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程LLM智能体依赖外部执行支持来维护状态、追踪进度、调用工具、验证结果和复用经验。然而，有效使用harness面临两个耦合挑战：从嘈杂交互轨迹中形成状态，以及对外部状态访问的运行时控制。现有方法通常通过提示、启发式或领域特定约定处理，导致外部工作空间及其使用策略依赖人工设计而非学习获得。

Method: 提出harness策略学习问题，即智能体离线学习harness策略并在运行时在线部署以构造和更新外部状态。具体包括：(1) 引入Belief、Progress、Experience (BPE)三种策略面向的harness状态；(2) 监督式harness微调教会基础智能体harness动作空间及如何构造有用的外部状态；(3) 成本感知的GRPO（近端策略优化变体）探索协调策略，在长程交互中选择性读取、更新和整合状态。

Result: 在ALFWorld上使用Qwen3-8B达到96.9%的成功率。揭示了两种关键动态：(1) harness退火——训练将重复的harness使用模式内化进模型策略，使智能体从频繁调用harness转向选择性外部状态访问；(2) harness演化——进度更新和经验整合将harness精炼为紧凑、任务自适应的状态基底。
