The resulting architecture offers hospital leaders a pragmatic blueprint to move from ad hoc tools to a governed, globally compliant, ROI-focused AI platform that can be tailored to on-premise, hybrid and cloud-native deployments.

</details>


### [265] [Mind the Gaps: Mixture-of-Minds for Human Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06115)
*Pranav Dahiya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Anacreon，一个针对特定领域个体级别的受众模拟模型。它通过学习作者嵌入将个体分离，对真实定性语料按种子人物聚类，并为每个簇训练专用适配器（混合智能体），基于Gemma 4 12B基础模型构建。模型从公开文本中提取人口统计学、心理特质和调查响应，并为每条记录添加情感链推理。通过打乱选项顺序降低提示脆弱性，通过平衡训练分布降低正向偏差。在外部大规模调查上达到0.775的序数对齐（个体级准确率），实现最先进水平且残差偏差较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型模拟器能够在群体层面恢复总体中心趋势，但在个体层面存在显著不足：它们扁平化人群异质性、携带社会偏见，且对提示措辞敏感，导致个体预测失真。统计方法在个体层面同样失效，因此迫切需要一种能够在明确限定的领域内实现个体层面精准模拟的方法。

Method: Anacreon采用四条核心策略：(1) 学习作者嵌入以区分不同个体；(2) 对真实定性语料按种子人物进行聚类并应用"mixture of minds"方法，为每个簇训练专用适配器（基于Gemma 4 12B）；(3) 从公开文本中收获人口统计学、心理特质和调查响应，并为每条记录补充chain-of-emotion情感链推理；(4) 通过打乱回答选项降低提示脆弱性，通过平衡训练分布减少正向响应偏差。

Result: 在外部大规模调查数据集上，Anacreon达到0.775的序数对齐分数（个体级准确率指标），刷新了该领域最先进水平，且仅残留较小的偏差。

Conclusion: Anacreon证明通过混合专家式适配器与结构化个体特征建模，可以有效弥合群体级与个体级模拟之间的鸿沟，是从忠实模拟个体中提取聚合洞察的重要一步。

Abstract: Predicting how a population will answer a new question is a long-standing goal. Statistical methods succeed at the level of the mass but falter at the level of the individual. Large language model simulators inherit this gap. They recover a population's central tendencies while flattening its heterogeneity, and they carry social biases and prompt brittleness that distort individual predictions. This paper introduces Anacreon, an audience simulation model that targets the individual level within a narrow, well-specified domain. Anacreon learns an authorship embedding that separates individuals, clusters a real qualitative corpus around seed people, and trains a dedicated adapter for each cluster, a mixture of minds, on a Gemma~4 12B base. It harvests demographics, psychological traits, and survey responses from public text, and augments each record with a chain-of-emotion. It reduces prompt brittleness by shuffling response options and reduces positive bias by balancing the training distribution. On a large, externally sourced survey, Anacreon reaches a state-of-the-art ordinal alignment of 0.775, the individual-level accuracy measure on which the field has converged, with a small residual bias. The work is a step toward drawing aggregate insight from faithfully simulated individuals.

</details>


### [266] [Poli-Bias: Understanding and Measuring Large Language Model Biases in International Political Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06123)
*Massi-Nissa Abboud,Aladin Djuhera,Elena Cabrio,Holger Boche*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Poli-Bias框架，通过反事实配对提示（系统性交换国家身份）来测量LLM在不同国家冲突场景下的政治偏见，将响应差异分解为五个可解释维度，并在13个当代LLM上验证了国家身份和用户归属会系统性影响模型对等效法律行为的描述、评估和辩护方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM中的政治偏见难以测量，因为它往往通过框架选择、论证方式和法律推理中的细微差异表现出来，单一指标难以捕捉这类微妙的不平等对待。

Method: 引入Poli-Bias反事实框架：设计成对提示，在多样化地缘政治关系、法律违规和推理任务中系统性交换国家身份，并将响应差异分解为五个可解释维度，以揭示不平等对待的表现方式和位置。

Result: 在13个跨越不同模型家族和规模的当代LLM上，发现国家身份和用户归属可以系统性影响模型对国际法下等效行为的描述、评估和辩护。

Conclusion: Poli-Bias可作为一种细粒度框架，用于审计LLM的政治公正性（even-handedness）和谄媚行为（sycophancy）。

Abstract: Measuring political bias in large language models (LLMs) remains challenging as it can manifest through subtle differences in framing, argumentation, and legal reasoning that are difficult to capture with a single metric. In this work, we introduce Poli-Bias, a counterfactual framework for measuring whether LLMs treat legally equivalent conflict scenarios differently depending on the countries involved. Poli-Bias compares responses to paired prompts in which country identities are systematically swapped across diverse geopolitical relationships, legal violations, and reasoning tasks. Rather than reducing bias to a single judgment, our framework decomposes response disparities into five interpretable dimensions, revealing how and where unequal treatment manifests. Across 13 contemporary LLMs spanning diverse model families and sizes, we find that country identities and user affiliations can systematically affect how equivalent actions are described, evaluated, and defended under international law. Our results thus establish Poli-Bias as a fine-grained framework for auditing political even-handedness and sycophancy in LLMs.

</details>


### [267] [Contextual Information Policy Optimization for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06128)
*Xingyu Guo,Wei Chen,Linlin Yang,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CIPO（上下文信息策略优化），一种面向证据导向的强化学习框架，通过密集的回合级信用分配显式对齐策略优化与外部证据使用，减少先验驱动推理（确认偏误），在七个领域内和跨域基准上获得优秀性能，且无需人工过程标注或额外奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要奖励最终答案正确性或中间进展，没有直接评估检索后动作是否基于检索证据。这种错位鼓励了先验驱动推理：智能体基于内部知识形成结论，检索仅用于确认，导致确认偏误和证据利用效率低下。

Method: CIPO将密集的回合级信用分配给受检索信息影响的推理动作，同时将证据使用信号与全局结果奖励相结合以保持答案正确性。该框架无需人工过程标注或额外奖励模型。

Result: 在七个领域内和跨域基准上的大量实验表明，CIPO减少了先验驱动推理的普遍性，并在大多数任务上取得了优秀的性能。

Conclusion: CIPO通过显式对齐策略优化与外部证据使用，有效抑制了证据脱离式猜测，促进了检索事实引导或修正后续推理的轨迹，为搜索智能体在知识密集型任务中的可靠性提供了改进方案。

Abstract: Search agents extend large language models beyond static parametric memory by enabling them to acquire and use ex ternal evidence during multi-step reasoning. For knowledge intensive tasks involving complex or evolving information, their reliability depends not only on retrieving relevant ev idence but also on using it to guide subsequent reasoning. However, existing methods primarily reward final-answer cor rectness or intermediate progress, without directly assessing whether post-retrieval actions are grounded in the retrieved evidence. This misalignment encourages prior-driven reason ing: agents form conclusions based on internal knowledge and use retrieval mainly to confirm them, resulting in confirma tion bias and inefficient evidenceuse.Toaddressthisissue, we propose Contextual Information Policy Optimization (CIPO), an evidence-oriented reinforcement learning framework that explicitly aligns policy optimization with external evidence use. CIPO assigns dense, turn-level credit to reasoning ac tions influenced by retrieved information, while combining this evidence-use signal with a global outcome reward to pre serveanswercorrectness.Withthismanner,CIPOdiscourages evidence-detached guesses and promotes reasoning trajecto ries in which retrieved facts can guide or revise subsequent reasoning. Importantly, CIPO requires neither human process annotations nor an additional reward model. Extensive exper iments on seven in-domain and out-of-domain benchmarks show that CIPO reduces the prevalence of prior-driven rea soning and achieves excellent performance on most tasks.

</details>


### [268] [FinEvo-Bench: A Longitudinal Benchmark for Self-Evolving Agents in Professional Financial Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06144)
*Bo Deng,Kang Zhou,Lifan Guo,Chongyang Tao,Xuanren Chen,Chenggang Xie,Renzhao Liang,Feng Chen,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FinEvo-Bench，一个包含120个真实案例、覆盖六个金融领域20个业务场景的纵向基准，用于评估智能体能否将先前任务经验转化为后续任务改进。实验表明，基于Qwen3.7-Max骨架的四个自进化智能体支架中，Letta达到最高进化分数（91.65）和最少合规问题，Codex获得最大的自进化增益（+19.37），且进化条件普遍优于非进化条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准大多是独立评估任务，无法衡量一个任务的经验是否有助于后续任务；现有的自进化基准也未同时覆盖专业工作流、开放式交付物和多维度评估。因此需要一个新的纵向基准来专门衡量智能体的自进化能力。

Method: FinEvo-Bench包含120个基于真实案例的任务，涵盖六个金融领域20个业务场景。机构提供的专业流程定义所需操作和约束，符合条件的机构提供和公开文档案例提供任务事实。每个场景包含六个共享专业流程和人工审核评分标准的相关但实质性不同的案例。实验比较了四种使用相同Qwen3.7-Max骨干的自进化智能体支架，在三个独立洗牌、全局交错的任务流上进行测试，配对非进化对照组估算自进化增益，使用Claude Code评分代理（Claude Opus 4.6支持）评估所有输出。

Result: Letta达到最高进化分数91.65和最少合规问题（每任务0.09）；Codex实现最大自进化增益+19.37。所有支架中，进化条件使分数提高9.33-19.37分，每任务合规问题减少0.12-0.44个。场景内排名4-6的配对得分增益比排名1-3高6.10-8.70分。在Claude Code中，仅技能进化优于仅记忆进化和记忆-技能联合进化。所有支架中，rubric反馈优于参考答案反馈。

Conclusion: FinEvo-Bench能同时衡量专业表现和自进化能力——即智能体如何有效地将先前经验转化为后续改进。研究发现自进化条件在任务质量和合规性上均显著优于非进化条件，且技能进化、rubric反馈是更有效的设计选择。

Abstract: Most agent benchmarks evaluate tasks independently and cannot measure whether experience from one task helps with later tasks. Existing self-evolution benchmarks do not jointly cover professional workflows, open-ended deliverables, and multi-aspect evaluation. We introduce FinEvo-Bench, a longitudinal benchmark with 120 real-case-grounded tasks, 20 business scenes across six financial domains. Institution-provided professional procedures define the required operations and constraints. Eligible institution-provided and publicly documented cases supply the task facts. Each scene contains six related but substantively distinct cases that share a professional procedure and a manually reviewed rubric for task quality and financial compliance. We compare four self-evolving agent scaffolds using the same Qwen3.7-Max backbone and three independently shuffled, globally interleaved task streams. Paired non-evolving controls estimate each scaffold's self-evolution gain from retained experience, while an independent Claude Code scoring agent backed by Claude Opus 4.6 evaluates all outputs. Letta achieves the highest evolved score (91.65) and fewest compliance issues (0.09 per task); Codex achieves the largest self-evolution gain (+19.37). Across scaffolds, the evolving condition raises scores by 9.33-19.37 points and reduces compliance issues by 0.12-0.44 per task. Paired score gains at within-scene ranks 4-6 exceed those at ranks 1-3 by 6.10-8.70 points. In Claude Code, skill-only evolution produces higher task quality and fewer compliance issues than memory-only and combined memory-skill evolution. Across all four scaffolds, rubric feedback also yields higher scores and fewer compliance issues than reference-answer feedback. FinEvo-Bench measures both professional performance and self-evolution ability: how effectively an agent turns prior experience into later improvement.

</details>


### [269] [PaDoc: Layout-Grounded Parallel Decoding for Document Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06146)
*Hao Yu,Jiabo Zhan,Kang Liu,Linnan Zhao,Dongxu Yue,Rui Chen,Jinglin Wang,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PaDoc是一种基于布局引导的端到端文档解析器，将预测的布局视为共享页面表示上的分支结构，通过前缀条件分解实现布局流和区域内容分支的并发推进，将解码深度降至最长布局-内容路径。在OmniDocBench Full上达到91.1的布局F1和94.24的Overall得分，相比同骨干顺序微调基线，吞吐量提升67.4-118%，P95延迟降低39.2-54.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端文档解析器将页面布局和区域内容序列化为单个自回归序列，导致独立区域被迫沿着随总内容增长的解码路径生成；而两阶段裁剪式解析器虽然支持区域级并行，但需要重复的视觉预填充且页面上下文碎片化。目标是在保留完整页面上下文的同时消除依赖。

Method: PaDoc提出将布局作为共享页面表示上的分支结构。在区域充分性假设下，推导出前缀条件分解，使布局流与区域内容分支并发推进，将解码深度缩短至最长布局-内容路径。实现上在单一多模态大语言模型(MLLM)中完成：采用打包的可变长度祖先注意力以在标准next-token训练下保持可见性；通过掩码并行解码创建分支，评估的vLLM后端以并发请求方式提供服务，并复用缓存驻留的共享前缀。

Result: 在OmniDocBench Full上，PaDoc达到91.1的Overall布局F1，端到端解析器中Overall得分为94.24（顶级），Text Edit为0.038（最优），Formula CDM为95.59（最优）。在384页子集和单张A800 GPU上，五个并发级别下是最快的端到端解析器，有效页面吞吐量相对同骨干顺序微调基线提升67.4-118%，P95延迟降低39.2-54.9%。

Conclusion: PaDoc通过布局引导的分支解码结构，成功在不牺牲全页面上下文的前提下实现区域级并行，兼顾了解析质量和推理效率，验证了将布局作为分支条件分解的可行性。

Abstract: End-to-end document parsers provide a unified interface, but serialize page layouts and regional contents into one autoregressive sequence. This formulation forces independent regions onto a decoding path whose length grows with the total content, whereas crop-based two-stage parsers expose region-level parallelism at the cost of repeated visual prefills and fragmented page context. To retain full-page context while removing dependencies, we propose PaDoc, a layout-grounded parser that treats the predicted layout as a branching structure over a shared page representation. Under a region-sufficiency assumption, we derive a prefix-conditioned factorization in which the layout stream and regional content branches advance concurrently, reducing the decoding depth to the longest layout-content path. We realize this factorization within a single MLLM: packed variable-length ancestor attention preserves the visibility under standard next-token training, while masked parallel decoding creates branches that the evaluated vLLM backend serves as concurrent requests with cache-resident shared-prefix reuse. On OmniDocBench Full, PaDoc attains an Overall layout F1 of 91.1 and, among end-to-end parsers, a top-tier Overall score of 94.24 together with the best Text Edit (0.038) and Formula CDM (95.59). On a 384-page subset and one A800 GPU, it is the fastest end-to-end parser at five concurrency levels, improving valid-page throughput by 67.4-118% and reducing P95 latency by 39.2-54.9% relative to a same-backbone Sequential SFT baseline. Code is available at https://github.com/Longin-Yu/Padoc

</details>


### [270] [CogVis: Must Open-Vocabulary Change Detection Perceive the Scene Anew for Every Query?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06150)
*Zijie Wang,Chen Zhong,Wei He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CogVis是一个认知记忆引导的开放词汇变化检测框架，将OVCD重构为"感知-记忆-验证"范式，通过场景变化感知器提取类别无关的变化先验、语义记忆校准器动态估计决策阈值、自适应区域过滤器筛选候选区域，在七个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能，并将推理吞吐量提升28.50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇变化检测方法往往将时间感知、语义判别和区域验证纠缠在一起，导致结果不稳定且存在冗余计算。作者受人类视觉变化感知机制的启发，希望通过解耦这三个过程来构建更稳定、更高效的OVCD框架。

Method: 提出CogVis框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 场景变化感知器(SCP)，从冻结的双时相特征中提取可复用的、类别无关的变化先验，实现时间证据与语义类别决策的解耦；(2) 语义记忆校准器(SMC)，通过动态估计图像-查询特定的决策阈值来补偿类别相关的分数偏移；(3) 自适应区域过滤器(ARF)，利用学习到的语义、时间和结构可靠性来过滤连通候选区域。

Result: 在涵盖语义变化检测、二值变化定位和建筑损伤评估的七个基准数据集上，CogVis在所有数据集上均取得最先进的性能。通过共享场景级变化感知，避免了跨查询重复进行类别无关的时间感知，推理吞吐量提升28.50%。

Conclusion: CogVis通过认知记忆引导的感知-记忆-验证范式，有效解决了OVCD中时间感知、语义判别与区域验证相互纠缠的问题，在性能和效率上均实现了显著提升，为开放词汇变化检测提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Earth-surface monitoring requires change detection models capable of recognizing arbitrary semantic categories. Open-Vocabulary Change Detection (OVCD) addresses this need. However, existing methods often entangle temporal perception, semantic discrimination, and region verification, causing unstable results and redundant computation. Inspired by human visual change perception, we propose CogVis, a cognitive memory-guided framework that reformulates OVCD as a perception-memory-verification paradigm. CogVis first employs a Scene Change Perceptron (SCP) to extract a reusable, category-agnostic change prior from frozen bi-temporal features, thereby decoupling temporal evidence from semantic category decisions. A Semantic Memory Calibrator (SMC) then compensates for category-dependent score shifts by dynamically estimating an image-query-specific decision threshold. Finally, an Adaptive Region Filter (ARF) filters connected candidates using learned semantic, temporal, and structural reliability. Experiments on seven benchmarks spanning semantic change detection, binary change localization, and building-damage assessment show that CogVis achieves state-of-the-art performance across all evaluated datasets. By sharing scene-level change perception, CogVis further avoids repeating category-agnostic temporal perception across queries and improves inference throughput by 28.50%.

</details>


### [271] [iARCS: Iterative Agentic RL for Controllable 3D Scene Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06161)
*Saugat Adhikari,Ashok Prasad Neupane,Pramish Paudel,Ajad Chhatkuli,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: iARCS是一个迭代式智能体强化学习框架，用于将预训练的3D场景生成器适配到自然语言任务需求上。它采用两阶段策略：通用奖励预训练（提升物理合理性和布局质量）和任务特定微调（利用LLM生成的奖励程序并根据训练反馈迭代优化）。实验证明在可走性、可达性和净空等任务上约束保真度提升，同时保持场景多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的合成3D场景生成器往往只优化感知层面的真实感，而未能可靠地满足任务关键的功能性约束（如可达性、可通行性、空间规则符合性），这限制了合成数据在下游训练中的实用价值。

Method: iARCS框架采用两阶段策略：（1）通用奖励预训练阶段，提升场景的物理合理性和布局质量；（2）任务特定微调阶段，使用LLM生成的奖励程序（reward programs）并根据训练反馈迭代优化这些奖励程序，从而实现迭代式智能体强化学习。

Result: 实验表明iARCS在可走性（walkability）、可达性（reachability）和净空（clearance）相关任务上显著提升了约束保真度，实现了有效的任务特定约束优化，同时保持了有竞争力的场景多样性。此外，iARCS生成的数据还能改进基础生成器本身，证明其作为实际合成数据生成工具的价值。

Conclusion: iARCS不仅是一个可控的场景编辑方法，更是一个实用的合成数据生成工具。通过迭代强化学习将场景生成器与自然语言任务需求对齐，有效弥合了感知真实感与任务功能约束之间的差距。

Abstract: Synthetic 3D scene generation is increasingly used as a data source for computer vision and embodied AI, but existing generators often optimize perceptual realism without reliably satisfying task-critical functional constraints. This mismatch limits the usefulness of synthetic data for downstream training, where accessibility, traversability, and spatial rule compliance are often essential. We present iARCS, an iterative agentic reinforcement learning framework that adapts a pretrained scene generator to natural-language task requirements. iARCS uses a two-stage strategy: universal-reward pretraining to improve physical plausibility and layout quality, followed by task-specific fine-tuning with LLM-generated reward programs that are iteratively refined from training feedback. Experiments show improved constraint fidelity on walkability, reachability, and clearance-focused tasks, effective task-specific constraint optimization, and competitive scene diversity. We further show that data generated by iARCS improves a base generator, supporting its value as a practical synthetic data generation tool rather than only a controllable scene editing method.

</details>


### [272] [Schema-Guided Hierarchical Information Extraction and Semantic Evaluation Using Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06167)
*Modhurita Mitra,Jan-Willem Versteeg,Maarten D. Schermer,Shiva Nadi Najafabadi,Marie L. De Bruin,Lourens T. Bloem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于模式（schema）的框架，利用生成式AI从非结构化文本中提取复杂结构化信息，并通过路径语义匹配算法和生成式AI进行自动化语义评估。在NICE健康技术评估文档中，14个属性中有12个F1分数超过90%，提取速度比人类专家快约30倍，并展示了框架跨模型、跨组织和跨语言的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非结构化文本中提取复杂、嵌套的结构化信息（如层级属性、可变基数属性）是一个具有挑战性的任务。现有方法缺乏统一的框架来同时支持信息提取和提取结果的评估，需要一种系统化、一致性的方法。

Method: 论文提出一个基于模式（schema）的信息提取框架：1）模式作为编码领域知识的信息模型，支持层级嵌套和可变基数属性的提取；2）零样本模式下一次调用生成式AI模型完成整个文档的信息提取；3）引入路径式语义匹配算法来对齐提取结果与金标准中的嵌套可变属性；4）利用生成式AI对属性值进行语义比较，并提出一个包含精确匹配(exact)、语义匹配(semantic)、有用匹配(useful)和非匹配(non-match)四个等级的评价标准。

Result: 在NICE健康技术评估文档上，使用Claude Opus 3模型，14个属性中有12个达到F1>90%；单文档提取时间比人类领域专家快约30倍；框架在不同生成式AI模型、不同HTA组织和不同语言之间展示了良好的可迁移性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该框架通过模式驱动的方式结合生成式AI，能够高效、准确地从非结构化文本中提取复杂结构化信息，并提供可靠的自动化语义评估机制，具有跨模型、跨组织、跨语言的广泛适用性。

Abstract: We present a schema-based framework for extracting complex, structured information from unstructured text documents using generative AI, followed by automated semantic evaluation of the extracted information against a gold standard. The schema, serving as an information model encoding domain knowledge, provides a unified, systematic, and consistent framework for extraction of hierarchical, nested information, with attributes of variable cardinality, and subsequent evaluation of the results. Information extraction from a document is performed in a single call to the model, in zero-shot mode.
  In the evaluation step, we introduce a path-based semantic matching algorithm to align the nested, variable-cardinality attributes in the extracted results with those in the gold standard. We use generative AI for semantic comparison of the extracted and gold standard values of an attribute, and introduce a rubric to classify the result of the comparison, according to domain-specific considerations, as an exact, semantic, useful, or non-match.
  We were able to extract 12 out of 14 attributes with an F1 score of $>$90\% from documents published by the health technology assessment organisation NICE, using the generative AI model Claude Opus 3. The time needed to extract the attributes from a document was $\sim$30 times lower than the time taken by a human domain expert. We further demonstrate generalisability of this framework across different generative AI models and transferability across different HTA organisations and languages.

</details>


### [273] [MicroEvo: Knowledge-Guided LLM Sampling for Efficient Microarchitecture Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06183)
*Jia Xiong,Runkai Li,Chenxu Niu,Guangyuan Gao,Changwen Xing,Yifan Zhang,Xinlai Wan,Jieran Cui,Chen Bai,Yusheng Hua,Ying Wang,Ming Ling,Xi Wang,Tao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MicroEvo是一个知识驱动的微架构设计空间探索框架，结合大语言模型与蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）进行多目标微架构优化。相比NSGA-II，Pareto前沿质量提升最高36.2%，搜索效率提升10.6倍，并展现出对工业级复杂核的强扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微架构设计空间探索面临搜索空间庞大和PPA评估昂贵的双重挑战，可用仿真预算非常有限。现有方法存在两大缺陷：一是盲搜索，不考虑微架构组件间的依赖关系；二是无法从迭代搜索过程中有效学习，导致评估资源浪费和Pareto收敛性差。

Method: MicroEvo采用知识驱动框架，将现成LLM与MCTS耦合用于多目标微架构优化。核心组件包括：1) LLM驱动的进化算子（用于生成候选微架构配置）；2) Pareto感知的树策略（平衡Pareto贡献与多样性）；3) 主动知识积累机制（提取并复用优化洞察）；4) 状态感知指令（在线自适应调整搜索行为）。

Result: 实验表明，MicroEvo在Pareto前沿质量上相比NSGA-II最高提升36.2%，搜索效率提升10.6倍，并且在复杂工业级核心上展现出强扩展性。代码已开源。

Conclusion: MicroEvo验证了将LLM与MCTS结合用于微架构设计空间探索的有效性，通过知识积累与自适应搜索策略显著改善了多目标优化的收敛性和效率，为大规模微架构设计空间探索提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Microarchitecture design space exploration suffers from expansive search spaces and expensive PPA evaluation, leaving only a small simulation budget for design decision-making. Existing methods perform blind search without considering microarchitectural dependencies and fail to learn from the iterative search effectively, leading to wasted evaluations and weak Pareto convergence. In this paper, we propose MicroEvo, a knowledge-guided framework that couples off-the-shelf LLMs with Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) for multi-objective microarchitecture optimization. MicroEvo combines LLM-driven evolutionary operators, a Pareto-aware tree policy that balances Pareto contribution and diversity, an active knowledge accumulation mechanism that extracts and reuses optimization insights, and state-aware directives that adapt the search behavior online. Experiments show that MicroEvo improves Pareto-front quality by up to 36.2% over NSGA-II and achieves 10.6x higher search efficiency, and also demonstrates strong scalability to a complex industrial-scale core. The code repository is available at: https://github.com/GEAR-SEU/MicroEvo-ICCAD-26.

</details>


### [274] [Comparative Approaches to Agent Retrieval over Large Skill Libraries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06196)
*Indivara Kolluru,Nathan Sportsman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在大规模技能库场景下，混合检索器（词法与稠密嵌入）在top-5内达到73.5%的命中率，而类型化知识图谱相比检索器反而显著更差（-11.2分），原因在于图谱的边源自与检索器相同的嵌入邻域，存在预过滤拓扑束缚，无法扩展检索范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具备大规模技能库的智能体需要决定加载哪些技能及其顺序，但将整个库加载进上下文成本高昂且缺乏自主排序结构。需要研究稀疏按需加载与结构化图谱两种方案哪种更有效。

Method: 在690个技能的语料上，比较两种系统：（1）混合排序器，结合词法与稠密嵌入检索实现稀疏按需加载；（2）类型化知识图谱，编码前提条件、数据流和排序等工作流关系。在117个真实非回显查询上进行评估。

Result: 混合排序器在top-5内达到73.5%±8.0命中率；图谱按设计使用时显著更差（-11.2点，p=0.0007），LLM生成边层无额外增益，73%的排序器遗漏查询在图谱中根本不可达。98.6%的类型化边连接的技能是排序器已共同浮现的，图谱只能丰富关系语义但无法扩展检索范围。作者编写的查询会高估hit@5达44点。

Conclusion: 提供了一种机制性解释，说明为何在强排序器之上添加结构不会改善检索效果，并识别了将结构化相互依赖纳入检索为最优的条件。

Abstract: Agents backed by large skill libraries must decide which skills to load and in what order. Loading the entire library into context is expensive and provides no structure for autonomous sequencing. We study two systems for this problem over a corpus of 690 skills: a hybrid ranker combining lexical and dense-embedding retrieval for sparse, on-demand loading, and a typed knowledge graph encoding workflow relations such as prerequisites, data flow, and ordering. On a set of 117 realistic, non-echoing queries, the hybrid ranker retrieves the correct skill within the top five in 73.5% +/- 8.0 of cases, leaving roughly a quarter of queries unserved. When used as the design intended (substituting graph neighbours for additional ranked results at matched token budget), the graph is significantly worse (-11.2 points, p = 0.0007). Its LLM-generated edge layer adds nothing over neighbours obtained free from a local embedding pass, and 73% of the queries the ranker misses are not reachable through the graph at all. We attribute this to a pre-filter topology bound. Because the graph's candidate edges are drawn from the same embedding neighbourhood the ranker already searches, 98.6% of typed edges connect skills the ranker had already surfaced together. The graph can enrich relation semantics but cannot extend retrieval reach. We further show that evaluating on author-written queries overstates hit@5 by up to 44 points, which would have hidden these results entirely. Our contribution is a mechanistic account of why added structure does not improve retrieval over a strong ranker, and identify the conditions under which adding structural interdependence into the retrieval is optimal.

</details>


### [275] [EnvACE: Internalizing Environment Dynamics via World Rehearsal for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06197)
*Zishan Xu,Zhiyuan Yao,Yuxin Chen,Yifu Guo,Zhengxi Lu,Yuquan Lu,Jinyang Huang,Yan Xu,Yasheng Wang,Weinan Zhang,Xingshan Zeng,Weiwen Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnvACE是一种无需外部环境交互即可训练大语言模型智能体进行长期工具使用的强化学习方法，通过"世界排练"（world rehearsal）机制，让策略在训练中交替扮演行动者和环境角色，内部化动作与环境的响应关系，从而形成一个直接在模型参数中支持决策的agent世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的大语言模型智能体用于长时间跨度工具使用的训练通常依赖真实或合成的可执行环境交互，但这类环境的构建与验证成本高昂；外部模拟器又难以与真实世界对齐。作者希望找到一种摆脱外部环境依赖的训练范式，以更低成本扩展LLM智能体训练。

Method: 提出EnvACE方法，这是一种基于世界排练的智能体强化学习方法。训练过程中不进行外部环境交互，而是让策略交替执行"行动"与"排练"两个角色：先生成工具调用，再扮演环境回应该动作所引发的响应，并基于排练出的响应继续做后续决策。两个角色通过任务成功奖励进行端到端联合优化，从而将动作-环境响应关系内化到模型参数中。

Result: 在BFCL-v4、tau^2-Bench、VitaBench和FinMCP-Bench四个基准上，EnvACE取得了强劲且可迁移的性能，整体评估中优于依赖环境扩展的基线方法。受控实验进一步表明世界排练在不同模型规模下均能一致地改善策略学习。在测试阶段，内化的世界模型可通过"私有排练"在正式执行前进行预演，在中等的排练预算下无需额外外部交互即可获得进一步的性能提升。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，世界排练为超越外部环境约束的LLM智能体训练开辟了一条新路径，不仅降低了训练对环境资源的依赖，还在多基准上实现了与甚至超越环境密集型方法的性能。

Abstract: Training large language model agents for long-horizon tool use typically relies on interactions with real or synthesized executable environments, whose construction and verification are costly, or on external simulators that are difficult to ground. We introduce EnvACE, an agentic reinforcement learning method that replaces external environment interaction during training with world rehearsal. The policy alternates between acting and rehearsal: it first generates a tool call, then plays the role of the environment to produce the response induced by that action, and conditions subsequent decisions on the rehearsed response. Both roles are jointly optimized end-to-end using task-success rewards. Through world rehearsal, the policy internalizes the relationship between actions and their environment responses in its parameters, yielding an agent world model that directly supports decision making. Across BFCL-v4, tau^2-Bench, VitaBench, and FinMCP-Bench, EnvACE achieves strong and transferable performance, outperforming environment-scaling baselines in the overall evaluation. Controlled studies further show that world rehearsal consistently improves policy learning across model scales. At test time, the internalized world model enables private rehearsal before committed execution, yielding further gains under a moderate rehearsal budget without additional external interaction. Our findings establish world rehearsal as a new path toward scaling LLM agent training beyond the constraints of external environments. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Within-yao/EnvACE.

</details>


### [276] [TS-RAG: Retrieval Augmented Generation for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06223)
*Yixiong Xiao,Congxi Xiao,Jingbo Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TS-RAG框架，将检索增强生成技术引入时间序列预测领域，通过专门设计的参考token有效融合输入序列与检索到的相似序列信息，在多个真实世界预测基准上取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RAG在大语言模型中已被证明能通过引入相关外部信息增强模型能力，但在时间序列预测领域应用有限。由于时间序列模型受限于训练数据少、参数规模小、缺乏大语言模型的生成能力，直接将参考序列拼接进输入（如语言模型的方式）效果不佳，因此需要专门设计一种适用于时间序列预测的RAG方法。

Method: 提出TS-RAG框架，核心是设计专门的参考token（reference tokens），用于有效融合输入序列与检索得到的相似时间序列片段的信息，从而更稳健地捕获复杂的时间动态特征。

Result: 在多个真实世界的时间序列预测基准上，TS-RAG实现了持续的最先进性能（consistent state-of-the-art performance）。

Conclusion: TS-RAG验证了检索增强策略在时间序列预测领域的可行性，通过设计的参考token机制克服了时间序列模型参数量小、生成能力有限等限制，为RAG在非语言模态任务中的应用提供了新的思路。

Abstract: While deep learning models, particularly transformer-based architectures, have shown impressive performance in time series forecasting, the application of retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) in this domain remains limited. Since RAG has proven effective in enhancing the capabilities of large language models by incorporating relevant external information, retrieving similar time series sequences as references might also improve accuracy in time series forecasting tasks. However, most time series models are constrained by limited training data, smaller parameter scales, and a lack of the extensive generative capabilities found in large language models. Simply concatenating reference sequences into the prompt, as done in language models, may not yield the expected results. To address these challenges, we propose a novel approach, TS-RAG, which leverages RAG to enhance forecasting performance. The framework introduces specially designed reference tokens to effectively fuse information from the input sequence with that from retrieved similar sequences, enabling a more robust capture of complex temporal dynamics. Experimental results demonstrate that TS-RAG achieves consistent state-of-the-art performance across several real-world forecasting benchmarks.

</details>


### [277] [DASH: Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons for On-Policy Self-Distillation of Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06243)
*ZhiYan Hou,Xinyu Tang,Hongyan An,Jianjin Zhang,Weizhen Wang,Yunyun Han,Gengsheng Li,Xiangzhao Hao,Haiyun Guo,Wenbin Hu,Jinqiao Wang,Yafeng Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DASH（Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons）方法，通过感知局部蒸馏信号与序列级均值之间的差距来适应性地调整token级监督权重，从而改进基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中的密集token级分布监督。在三个数学推理基准和三个模型规模上的实验表明，DASH在所有设置下均优于普通OPSD，且无需额外的前向传播开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR使用自动可验证的结果信号来提升大语言模型的推理能力，但这些信号通常稀疏且位于序列级。OPSD通过查询特权教师模型在student访问的前缀处提供密集的token级分布监督来缓解信号稀疏性，但标准OPSD未充分利用rollout的时间结构——它对所有局部散度分配相同的系数，忽略了相同散度幅值可能对应不同离散历史（即教师与学生之间差异的不同演化轨迹）这一事实，因此无法根据已实现的散度序列自适应调整token级权重。

Method: DASH将每个局部蒸馏信号与序列级均值之间的差距映射为自适应传播门（adaptive propagation gate），并利用这些门控制反向多步聚合（backward multi-step aggregation）。通过这种方式，DASH能够根据生成过程中局部散度的演化动态调整token级监督权重，从而更充分地利用rollout的时间结构。

Result: 在三个数学推理基准上、跨越三个模型规模的实验中，DASH在所有基准和所有规模下均优于匹配的vanilla OPSD重跑结果。由于DASH复用了OPSD已计算的教师和学生分布，其增益不需要额外的教师或学生前向传播。

Conclusion: DASH通过感知局部散度在生成过程中的演化历史来自适应地分配token级监督权重，有效解决了标准OPSD无法区分相同局部散度在不同时间上下文中的差异这一局限。该方法在不增加计算开销的前提下显著提升了推理能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models using automatically verifiable outcome signals, but these signals are typically sparse and at the sequence-level. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) mitigates this sparsity by querying a privileged teacher at student-visited prefixes and providing dense token-level distributional supervision. Although this dense supervision alleviates signal sparsity, we find that standard OPSD still underexploits the temporal structure of the rollout. It assigns every local divergence the same coefficient, regardless of its position or the divergence sequence in which it occurs. In on-policy autoregressive generation, the same divergence magnitude can follow different discrepancy histories, reflecting different evolutions of the mismatch between the teacher and student. Since the local scalar alone cannot distinguish these temporal contexts, standard OPSD cannot adapt its token-level weights to the realized discrepancy sequence. To address this limitation, we propose Divergence-Adaptive Supervision Horizons (DASH). DASH maps the gap between each local distillation signal and the sequence-level mean to an adaptive propagation gate and then uses these gates to control backward multi-step aggregation. By doing so, DASH adjusts token-level supervision weights according to how local divergences evolve during generation. Experiments on three mathematical reasoning benchmarks across three model scales show that DASH improves over our matched vanilla OPSD reruns on every benchmark at all three scales. DASH reuses the teacher and student distributions that OPSD already computes, so the gains require no additional teacher or student forward pass.
  Code: https://github.com/DBtxy/DASH-OPSD

</details>


### [278] [Improving the Realism of Synthetic Clinical Benchmarks Under Utility Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06265)
*Omid Bazgir,Md Nasir,Jacob Hoffman,Yang Yang,Manu Agrawal,Anusua Trivedi,Vinay Rao Dandin,Chris Gibbons,Christine Swisher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将合成临床基准的修订形式化为"效用约束下的现实性提升"问题，通过两个确定性修订方案显著改善基准的现实性指标（缺失结构、简洁性、结构合理性、人群对齐），同时保持操作效用底线，并证明基准内部现实性与源保真度是两个相关但不同的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成临床基准即使能通过效用检查，在结构上仍可能不现实。尤其在医疗健康这类隐私敏感场景中，由于运营数据难以获取，合成基准缺乏现实性验证手段，需要在不破坏已有下游效用检查的前提下改进基准质量。

Method: 将基准修订建模为效用约束下的现实性优化问题。在基于Synthea生成的患者数据构建的护理缺口基准上，使用与运营数据相同的下游流水线处理，通过四个维度（缺失结构、简洁性、结构合理性、人群对齐）测量现实性，并设计两个确定性修订方案（对比朴素密集化控制组）进行改进。

Result: 基线基准极端单薄：成对缺失率79.44%、仅12.75%的行可操作、38.94%的患者无可操作测量、前三token集中度达100.0%。两个确定性修订方案在保持效用底线的同时改善了这些指标，而朴素密集化控制未能消除不现实的模板化。此外发现内部基准现实性与聚合运营参考的源保真度是相关但不同的目标。

Conclusion: 合成基准质量应被显式优化，效用应被视为一个约束条件而非现实性的充分证据。

Abstract: Synthetic clinical benchmarks for enterprise AI agents can pass existing utility checks and still remain structurally unrealistic, especially in privacy-sensitive healthcare settings where operational data are hard to access. We study how to improve such benchmarks without breaking the downstream utility checks already used in practice.
  We formulate benchmark revision as utility-constrained realism improvement: dataset changes should increase realism while staying above an operational utility floor. We instantiate this idea on a care-gap benchmark derived from Synthea-generated patients exercised through demonstration electronic health record workflows and then processed by the same downstream pipeline as operational data. Realism is measured through missingness structure, simplicity, structural plausibility, and population alignment.
  The baseline benchmark is extremely thin: sampled-pair missingness is 79.44%, only 12.75% of rows are actionable, 38.94% of patients have zero actionable measures, and top-three token concentration reaches 100.0%. Two deterministic revisions improve these panels while remaining above the current utility floor, whereas a naive densification control preserves unrealistic templating. We further show that internal benchmark realism and source fidelity to an aggregate operational reference are related but distinct objectives. These results suggest that synthetic benchmark quality should be optimized explicitly, with utility treated as one constraint rather than as sufficient evidence of realism.

</details>


### [279] [The Illusion of Visual Tool-Use: A Causal Audit of Thinking with Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06270)
*Zhiheng Wang,Bo Peng,Lai Wei,Chaochao Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果审计框架CauAudit，用于评估多模态LLM中"以图像思考"（视觉工具调用，如裁剪和缩放）是否真正因果性地影响答案。通过三级干预（策略级、轨迹级、步骤级），发现视觉工具使用存在"幻觉"：尽管总体准确率有所提升，但大多数rollout中返回的视觉观测对答案并无因果效应，揭示了"不查看而调用"（Calling Without Looking）和"查看而不规划"（Looking Without Planning）两种失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态LLM通过"以图像思考"范式获得主动视觉操作能力（如裁剪放大），但这些操作往往收益甚微甚至为负，同时消耗大量token。模型可能反复裁剪无关区域，或在直接推理本可答对的问题上失败。现有评估仅关注聚合准确率，无法回答一个关键问题：返回的视觉证据是否在因果层面真正影响了答案？作者旨在区分观测介导的路径与动作引入的捷径，揭示视觉工具使用的因果有效性。

Method: 将视觉工具使用形式化为因果图，区分观测介导路径与动作诱发捷径。在三个层面进行干预审计：(1) 策略级：比较工具使用与直接推理；(2) 轨迹级：在rollout过程中破坏所有观测；(3) 步骤级：在固定前缀下反事实替换单个观测。步骤级估计量"Visual Evidence Gain"（视觉证据增益）隔离每个返回观测的因果贡献。在六个代表性模型和五个细粒度感知基准上进行实验验证。

Result: 发现策略级校准存在两种失败模式：Calling Without Looking（返回的观测对答案无因果效应）和Looking Without Planning（观测有信息量但调用时序不连贯）。轨迹级诊断将策略级准确率增益分解后发现，增益集中在少数"校准"子集中，绝大多数rollout中视觉工具使用并非因果有效。

Conclusion: 作者将聚合准确率增益与缺乏广泛因果效应之间的差距称为"视觉工具使用的幻觉"（illusion of visual tool-use）：尽管工具调用在聚合层面上提升了准确率，但在广泛的rollout范围内视觉工具使用并未实现因果有效性。这一发现为审计和改进视觉工具调用机制提供了新的因果视角。

Abstract: The "thinking-with-images" paradigm equips multimodal LLMs with active visual operations such as crop-and-zoom. However, models using these operations often achieve only marginal or negative gains over direct inference at substantially higher token cost. They may also repeatedly crop irrelevant regions and fail on questions that direct inference answers correctly. We ask whether the returned visual evidence causally affects the answer. To answer this question, we formulate visual tool-use as a causal graph that separates observation-mediated paths from action-induced shortcuts. We then audit it through interventions at the three levels: policy (comparing tool-use with direct inference), trajectory (corrupting all observations during rollout), and step (counterfactually replacing one individual observation under a fixed prefix). Our step-level estimand, Visual Evidence Gain, isolates the contribution of each returned observation. Across six representative models and five fine-grained perception benchmarks, we uncover policy miscalibration with two failure modes. In Calling Without Looking, returned observations have no causal effect on the answer. In Looking Without Planning, observations are informative but the call schedule is incoherent. A trajectory-level diagnostic decomposes the policy-level accuracy gain and shows that the gain is concentrated in a Calibrated minority. We term this discrepancy the illusion of visual tool-use: despite aggregate accuracy gains, visual tool-use is not causally effective across a broad range of rollouts. The code is available at https://github.com/OpenCausaLab/CauAudit.

</details>


### [280] [QuanTiMedAI: Quantum-Enhanced Time-Series Model guided by Agentic AI for Cardiac Arrest Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06294)
*Mutasim Fuad Sarker,Adiba Rahman Namira,Wafa Binte Alam,Md Adnan Arefeen,Mahzabeen Emu,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了QuanTiMedAI框架，一种用于ICU心脏骤停患者死亡预测的量子代理（quantum-agentic）系统，结合代理LLM进行临床特征发现和紧凑型量子循环网络进行时间序列建模，在MIMIC-IV数据集上仅用605个参数达到0.852的AUROC，比当前最先进基线提升约2.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICU心脏骤停患者的死亡率预测研究大多依赖入院早期的静态摘要特征，忽略了患者在整个ICU住院期间生理恶化与恢复的时间动态演变过程。作者旨在利用电子健康记录中丰富的时间序列数据，通过时间感知的建模方法来提升预测性能。

Method: QuanTiMedAI由两个核心组件构成：(1) 代理式大语言模型（agentic LLM）用于基于临床知识的特征发现与选择；(2) 紧凑型量子循环网络用于时间感知的死亡率预测。通过量子非线性特征增强在极低参数条件下实现竞争性的预测性能。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV心脏骤停患者队列上，QuanTiMedAI的量子增强架构仅用605个参数达到0.852的AUROC，相对当前最先进基线提升约2.9%。代理LLM引导的特征选择优于传统特征选择方法，消融实验系统验证了各架构设计选择的贡献。

Conclusion: 量子增强的序列建模在参数数量大幅减少的情况下仍能超越经典循环网络，证明量子技术在医学时间序列预测任务中的潜力，为ICU死亡预测提供了一种高效且准确的替代方案。

Abstract: Cardiac arrest remains one of the most lethal conditions encountered in intensive care units. Despite the growing availability of electronic health record data, existing mortality prediction studies in this population largely depend on static summaries derived from early admission. Such approaches ignore the temporal progression of physiological deterioration and recovery that unfolds throughout a patient's ICU stay. To address this limitation, we introduce QuanTiMedAI, a quantum-agentic framework developed for cardiac arrest mortality prediction using agentic AI guided quantum enhancement time series model. The proposed system combines an agentic large language model (LLM) for clinically informed feature discovery with a compact quantum recurrent network for temporality aware mortality prediction. Our findings demonstrate that agentic LLM-guided feature selection consistently outperforms conventional feature selection approaches, and the proposed quantum architecture achieves competitive predictive performance through nonlinear feature enhancement while keeping the number of parameters very low. Through extensive experimentation on a MIMIC-IV cohort of cardiac arrest patients, QuanTiMedAI's quantum-enhanced architecture attains an AUROC of 0.852 using only 605 parameters, an improvement of approximately 2.9\% over a current state-of-the-art baseline for this task. A structured ablation study systematically validates the contribution of each architectural design choice. These results show that quantum-enhanced sequential modeling can exceed classical recurrent networks while using substantially fewer parameters.

</details>


### [281] [Bias Analysis of L2 Speaking Assessment Systems Using Concept Activation Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06300)
*Arya Labroo,Mengjie Qian,Kate Knill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文扩展了概念激活向量（CAV）分析方法，将其应用于基于BERT和Whisper的两种神经口语评估系统，并引入稀疏自编码器（SAE）来改善概念方向的可线性恢复性。研究发现概念的可恢复性和对分数的影响取决于模型表示和架构，而非概念本身；SAE虽能提高概念的可线性恢复性，但会减弱原始激活空间中的概念敏感性，凸显了审计说话评估系统时需要区分"概念可恢复性"与"概念影响力"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动口语评估系统在高风险场景中越来越多地被用于为二语学习者的口语测试打分，必须证明其分数取决于口语能力而非与说话者无关的属性（如母语或年龄）。基于Transformer的基础模型提升了这些评分器的准确性，但其黑盒表示使公平性和可解释性分析变得更加困难。

Method: 在先前基于特征评分器使用CAV检测对不需要属性（"概念"）的偏见的基础上，将CAV分析扩展到两种神经口语评估系统：基于文本的BERT评分器和基于Whisper的语音+文本多模态评分器。CAV将人类可解释的概念表示为模型激活空间中的方向，可区分概念是否被编码在模型内部表示中，以及概念是否影响预测分数（后者通过梯度敏感性度量量化）。此外，由于CAV依赖线性可分性，在复杂神经嵌入空间中可能不成立，本文还研究了SAE是否通过在学习稀疏潜在空间中的CAV并将其映射回激活空间来提供更干净的概念方向。

Result: 分析表明，概念的可恢复性强烈依赖于被探测的表示和架构，而非仅取决于概念本身；对概念的敏感性也依赖于架构。SAE使概念更可线性恢复，但会削弱原始激活空间中的敏感性，尤其是在低维层中。

Conclusion: 这些发现强调了在审计口语评估系统的偏见时，需要区分概念可恢复性与概念影响力。

Abstract: Automatic speaking assessment systems are increasingly deployed in high-stakes settings to mark second language (L2) learners' speaking tests, making it critical to show that their scores depend on speaking proficiency rather than irrelevant speaker attributes such as first language (L1) or age. Transformer-based foundation models have improved the accuracy of these L2 speaking graders, but their black-box representations make fairness and interpretability analysis more difficult. Building on prior work that used Concept Activation Vectors (CAVs) to detect bias towards unwanted attributes (`concepts') in feature-based graders, we extend CAV-based analysis to two neural speaking assessment systems: a text-based BERT grader and a speech-and-text multimodal grader based on Whisper. CAVs represent human-interpretable concepts as directions in a model's activation space, allowing us to distinguish between whether a concept is encoded in a model's internal representations and whether it influences the predicted score, the latter quantified using a gradient-based sensitivity metric. Since CAVs rely on linear separability, which is less likely in complex neural embedding spaces, we also investigate whether sparse autoencoders (SAEs) provide cleaner concept directions by learning CAVs in a sparse latent space and mapping them back to activation space. Our analysis shows that concept recoverability depends strongly on the representation and architecture being probed, rather than on the concept alone. Sensitivity to concepts is also architecture-dependent. SAEs make concepts more linearly recoverable, but attenuate the original activation-space sensitivity, especially in low-dimensional layers. These findings highlight the need to distinguish concept recoverability from concept influence when auditing bias in speaking assessment systems.

</details>


### [282] [HarnessOpt-Bench: Evaluating LLMs at Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06301)
*Varun Ursekar,Apaar Shanker,Yash Maurya,Shehab Yasser,Vijay S. Kalmath,Veronica Chatrath,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HarnessOpt-Bench，一个用于端到端"模型外层框架优化"（harness optimization）的基准测试，该任务由LLM作为优化器在有限评估预算下迭代改进目标代理的提示词、工具、控制流等外围代码。基准在5个前沿LLM和4个下游任务上进行了111次评分的实验，结果表明优化器模型的差异比其使用的编码框架更能区分性能，且当前模型在该能力上有很大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代理系统中的能力不仅取决于模型权重，还取决于其外围框架（harness），包括提示词、工具、控制流、记忆和编排代码。这使得自动化的框架优化成为提升AI系统的重要途径，但社区缺乏一个统一的协议来衡量前沿LLM在此任务上的表现。

Method: 引入HarnessOpt-Bench基准：优化器（LLM配编码框架）接收目标代理的种子框架、分级评估反馈和固定评估预算，编辑框架并提名最终候选。候选由其在隐藏测试集上相对于种子的归一化增益评分。通过可信执行环境强制执行评估边界、计量目标代理资源使用并保留候选版本用于审计。在共享编码框架和原生框架两种条件下评估5个前沿LLM优化器，覆盖4个下游任务，共111次评分运行。

Result: 实验结果显示：优化器模型之间的差异比它们所借助的编码框架更能区分性能；原生框架并不始终优于共享框架；增益在不同任务和种子设置下差异显著。这些结果确立框架优化是一个可测量、有区分度的能力，且仍有很大的提升空间。

Conclusion: HarnessOpt-Bench为框架优化能力提供了首个标准化测评协议，证明该能力是可测量的且当前前沿模型表现尚有不足，为后续研究提供了基准和方向。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed within agentic systems, their capabilities depend not only on the model weights but also on the harness: the prompts, tools, control flow, memory, and orchestration code surrounding them. This makes automated harness optimization -- the iterative and evaluation-guided improvement of a harness by an AI system -- both an important route to improving AI systems and a demanding capability for AI systems themselves. Yet the community lacks a common protocol for measuring how well frontier LLMs perform at this task. We introduce HarnessOpt-Bench, a benchmark for end-to-end harness optimization under expensive and stochastic evaluation. An optimizer, an LLM paired with a coding harness, receives a target agent's seed harness, graded evaluation feedback, and a fixed target-evaluation budget. It edits the harness and nominates a final candidate, which is scored by its normalized gain over the seed on a held-out test partition that remains inaccessible throughout search. A trusted execution environment enforces the evaluation boundary, meters target-agent resource use, and preserves candidate versions for audit. We evaluate 5 frontier LLMs as optimizers both under a shared coding harness and under their native harnesses across 4 downstream tasks, over 111 scored runs. Experiment results show that optimizer models separate more than the coding harnesses they act through, native harnesses are not consistently superior, and gains vary substantially across tasks and seed regimes. These results establish harness optimization as a measurable and discriminative capability with large space for improvement.

</details>


### [283] [Beyond Top-K: Replacing Black-Box Retrieval with Interpretable Agentic Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06305)
*Sagar Tamang,Ayush Vyas,Tabarakul Hazarika*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出READ（Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search），一种面向长文档（特别是财务报告等表格密集型文档）的无需嵌入的代理式检索方案。作者论证了传统的"分块-嵌入-检索top-k"范式在表格密集型文档上结构性失效（如86.8%内容为表格行、数值继承头部单位的中位距离为13行），并提出通过三个确定性操作（归一化词法搜索、结构导航、有界跨度读取）实现文档搜索，产生可重放的审计轨迹。在51个验证问题上，READ准确率达58.8%，远超稠密检索的15.7%和top-k工具代理的27.5%，但BM25与其统计上无显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于财务报表、审计报告、监管申报等表格密集型长文档，传统的"分块-嵌入-top-k检索"设计在结构上不健全。具体而言，在780页政府财务报告中86.8%的内容行是表格行，数千个近似相同的数值竞争同一嵌入空间，且数值的单位继承自上方平均13行的表头——分块边界会例行地切断数值与其单位（如lakh与crore）的关联，造成两个数量级的误差。作者旨在量化并解决这一系统性缺陷。

Method: 作者首先构建了表格感知的分块器（作为"steelman"基线）来修复单位问题，但发现其在所有分块大小下仍有27-30%的数值块缺失财政年度表头。随后提出READ方法：使代理通过三个确定性操作读取原始文档——归一化词法搜索、结构导航和有界跨度读取，通过Model Context Protocol（MCP）暴露，使整个轨迹成为可重放的审计轨迹而非不透明的相似度分数。同时设计了对照实验（top-k工具代理）以区分迭代收益与界面收益。

Result: 在51个验证问题上，READ达到58.8%准确率，而稠密检索仅15.7%（Holm校正p=2x10^-5）；调优后稠密检索为35.3%，READ仍领先23.5个百分点（p=0.017）。采用相同循环但改用top-k工具的代理仅达27.5%，说明增益来自界面而非迭代。BM25与READ统计上无显著差异，表明结果区分的是基于嵌入与免嵌入的检索，而非代理式与词法式搜索。

Conclusion: 对于表格密集型长文档（如财务报告），基于嵌入的检索范式在结构上不充分，READ通过确定性的结构导航+词法搜索+有界读取的代理式设计在准确率上显著优于稠密检索和top-k工具变体。然而，BM25性能与READ相当，说明核心优势在于避免嵌入而非引入代理智能循环。作者还报告了证据不支持的部分：即代理式迭代本身优于词法搜索的结论。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over long documents is dominated by one design: chunk the text, embed the chunks, and surface the top-k nearest neighbours of the query. We argue that for an important class of documents -- financial statements, audit reports, regulatory returns -- this design is structurally unsound, and we make the argument measurable. On a 780-page government financial report, 86.8% of content lines are table rows, thousands of near-identical figures compete in one embedding space, and a figure inherits its unit from a header a median of 13 lines above it -- so a chunk boundary routinely separates a number from whether it is in lakh or crore, an error of two orders of magnitude. A table-aware chunker built as a steelman fixes the unit problem but leaves 27-30% of numeric chunks with no fiscal-year header at every chunk size we tried. We propose READ (Reliable Embedding-free Agentic Document-search), in which an agent reads the raw document through three deterministic operations -- normalized lexical search, structural navigation, and bounded span reads -- exposed over the Model Context Protocol, so a trajectory is a replayable audit trail, not an opaque similarity score. On 51 verified questions READ answers 58.8% against dense retrieval's 15.7% (p_Holm = 2 x 10^-5) -- or 35.3% tuned, which READ still leads by 23.5 points (p_Holm = 0.017). An agent given the same loop but a top-k tool reaches only 27.5%, locating the gain in the interface rather than in iteration. We also report what the evidence does not support: BM25 is statistically indistinguishable from READ, so our result separates embedding-based from embedding-free retrieval, not agentic from lexical search.

</details>


### [284] [TRAJDEBUG: Tracing Error Lifecycle to Identify Critical Failures in Long-Horizon Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06346)
*Yunjia Qi,Zehua Yin,Xintong Shi,Hao Peng,Songyuanyi Lu,Yixian Liu,Richeng Xuan,Yuhong Liu,Zhichao Hu,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Bin Xu,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajDebug，一个基于错误生命周期追踪的框架，用于在LLM智能体系统的长轨迹中定位导致最终失败的关键错误步骤；同时构建了包含486条人工标注失败轨迹的基准TrajErrBench，实验表明该方法优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统在复杂领域表现出色，但面临级联错误和调试困难的问题。现有关键错误检测面临两大挑战：一是长轨迹中判断错误的证据分散在遥远的指令、观察和先验上下文中，难以识别单个错误；二是失败轨迹通常包含多个局部错误且下游影响不同，只有部分错误对最终失败负责。

Method: TrajDebug框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 多粒度历史压缩与基于证据的错误识别，用于解决长轨迹中的错误发现；(2) 通过追踪每个错误的解决状态和终端影响，实现关键错误归因。

Result: 构建了TrajErrBench基准（486条来自Tau2Bench和SWE-Bench Pro的人工标注失败轨迹，涵盖工具使用和编码场景）。在多个智能体基准上的实验表明TrajDebug整体性能优于现有基线，应用研究进一步证明其诊断结果能为下游智能体改进提供可操作反馈。

Conclusion: TrajDebug在关键错误检测任务中取得了最佳性能，且诊断结果具有实际应用价值，可用于改进下游智能体成功率。代码和数据将公开发布以促进后续研究。

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems have shown remarkable capabilities in complex domains, while suffering from cascading errors and difficulty in debugging. Critical error detection aims to locate the earliest error step in a failed trajectory that is responsible for the final failure. However, progress faces two main challenges. First, long trajectories make it difficult to identify individual errors, since the evidence for judging a step may be scattered across distant instructions, observations, and prior context. Second, failed trajectories often contain multiple local errors with different downstream effects, only some of which remain responsible for the final failure. In this work, we propose TrajDebug, an error-lifecycle tracing framework that addresses long-trajectory error discovery with multi-granularity history compression and evidence-based error identification, and supports critical attribution by tracing each error's resolution status and terminal impact. We further construct TrajErrBench, a benchmark of 486 manually annotated failed trajectories from Tau2Bench and SWE-Bench Pro, covering realistic tool-use and coding scenarios. Experiments across diverse agent benchmarks show that TrajDebug achieves the best overall performance over existing baselines, and application studies further demonstrate that its diagnoses provide actionable feedback for improving downstream agent success. We will release the codes and data to facilitate further research.

</details>


### [285] [Challenges in Evaluating Explanation Methods for Static and Evolving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06351)
*Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有可解释人工智能（XAI）在评估方面的不足，通过DetoxAI图像识别系统（用于偏见检测和概念遗忘）进行说明；随后展示了人类基础评估用于解释图像分类的示例，并探讨了在概念漂移的流式数据环境中自适应解释（特别是反事实解释）的方法，最后将其关联到数据、模型与解释共同演化的追踪挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有XAI方法缺乏充分的评估机制，作者希望通过具体案例（DetoxAI系统）和人类基础评估来揭示这一局限，并探索在动态数据流（概念漂移）场景下解释的适应性问题。

Method: 论文采用案例驱动的分析方式：(1) 以DetoxAI图像识别系统为案例，展示偏见检测与概念遗忘中XAI评估的不足；(2) 开展人类基础评估实验，用于解释图像分类模型；(3) 探讨在概念漂移的数据流环境中自适应调整解释（特别是反事实解释）的方法。

Result: 论文通过DetoxAI系统演示了XAI评估不足的具体表现，给出了人类基础评估的示例，并讨论了反事实解释在概念漂移场景下的自适应实践经验，同时指出数据、模型与解释三者共同演化追踪所面临的挑战。

Conclusion: XAI需要更完善的评估框架，尤其在动态环境中解释必须能够自适应地演化；数据、模型和解释之间的协同演进是一个亟待深入研究的开放问题。

Abstract: This paper addresses the limitations of Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) with respect to insufficient evaluation. They are illustrated through the DetoxAI image recognition system for bias detection and concept unlearning. Then, an example of a human-grounded evaluation of methods for explaining image classification is presented. The paper further explores methods for adapting explanations to evolving data streams with concept drift. Experiences with adapting counterfactuals for this problem are discussed. Finally it is related to the challenges of tracking the co-evolution of data, models, and explanations.\footnote{This paper has been accepted for a publication in J.Nalepa (ed) Explainable AI in Space. Proceedings of EASi 2026 Workshop at IJCAI-ECAI 2026 Bremen, Springer CCIS vol 3107 (2016).}

</details>


### [286] [The Low Frequency Trap: Video Language Models Fail at Simple Event Bookkeeping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06361)
*Sarvesh Baskar,Zikui Cai,Shayan Shabihi,Anirudh Satheesh,Muhammad R. Islam,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可执行事件轨迹（trace-grounded）的参数化剖析方法，用于三个受控视频任务（弹球墙接触、视觉眨眼、类别状态转换）中的事件计数评估。通过2,190个视频，系统性地改变事件数量N和频率F，揭示了阶段性时间失败模式：瞬态事件（眨眼）缺乏可靠的正计数区域，而持续状态转换在低计数时可靠计数。在高计数高频率下，仅0.2%最终计数正确，事件恢复率仅18.1%。增加采样率虽能提升最终分数（19.6%→29.3%），但序列级一致性仅3.7%，说明额外帧会虚增分数而非产生忠实的事件恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界视频基准虽然覆盖广泛，但其固定片段将事件数量、速率、持续时间和视觉复杂性纠缠在一起，难以隔离失败模式。现有程序化基准虽提供更好的控制，但仅对最终答案评分，而非对照可执行的真实标签审计报告事件。需要一种能够精确诊断时间推理失败位置的方法。

Method: 作者引入trace-grounded参数化剖析方法：1) 设计三个受控视频任务（弹球墙接触、视觉眨眼、类别状态转换），在保持渲染固定的同时变化事件数量N和频率F；2) 每个视频附带可执行事件轨迹（executable event trace），支持能力表面估计和时间戳级评估；3) 通过2,190个视频进行系统化测量；4) 额外测试了提高采样率、不同提示策略以及真实世界视频评估作为对比。

Result: 在80%可靠性阈值下，Gemini 3.6 Flash可靠计数持续状态转换最多12个事件（0.5和1.0 Hz），但对瞬态眨眼事件无可靠正计数区域。高计数高频率下仅0.2%最终计数正确，恢复18.1%真实事件。提高采样率将Bounce Ball准确率从19.6%提升到29.3%，但序列与真实值一致仅3.7%。不同提示策略收益有限，真实世界视频评估同样显示成功集中在低事件数。

Conclusion: 事件表征方式决定了模型是否最初能访问证据——这一限制随计数和频率增加而加剧。额外帧可以虚增最终分数，但无法产生忠实的事件恢复。trace-grounded剖析将视频评估从聚合准确性指标转向详细诊断时间推理失败位置的范式。

Abstract: Real-world video benchmarks provide broad coverage, but their fixed clips entangle event count, rate, duration, and visual complexity, making failure modes hard to isolate. While existing programmatic benchmarks offer better control, they score only the final answer rather than auditing reported events against executable ground truth. To bridge this gap, we introduce trace-grounded parametric profiling for event counting in three controlled video tasks: bouncing-ball wall contacts, visual blinks, and categorical state transitions. Across 2,190 videos, we vary event count N and frequency F while holding rendering fixed. Each video includes an executable event trace for capability-surface estimation and timestamp-level evaluation. Our results reveal a staged temporal failure. At an 80% reliability threshold, Gemini 3.6 Flash reliably counts persistent state transitions up to 12 events at 0.5 and 1.0 Hz, yet demonstrates no reliable positive-count region for transient blinking events. Thus, event representation dictates whether a model initially accesses evidence -- a limitation that compounds as count and frequency increase. In the high-count, high-frequency regime, only 0.2% of final counts are correct and the model recovers just 18.1% of true events. To test if visual access is the primary bottleneck, we increase sampling rate. Although this boosts Bounce Ball accuracy from 19.6% to 29.3%, the reported sequence agrees with ground truth only 3.7% of the time. Extra frames can therefore inflate final scores without producing faithful event recovery. Different prompting strategies yield similarly limited gains, and real-world video evaluations show the same concentration of success at low event counts. Ultimately, trace-grounded profiling shifts video evaluation from aggregate accuracy metrics to a detailed diagnostic of where temporal reasoning fails.

</details>


### [287] [Tracing the Heart: An Evidence-Linked Pipeline for Heart-Failure Feature Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06366)
*Soorya Ram Shimgekar,Michelle Hu,Dorisa Shehi,Daniel Kang,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Koustuv Saha,Christian Poellabauer,Christopher Lee,Sajeev Singh,Piyum Zonooz,Navin Kumar,Zeeshan Ahmed,Priyadarshini Kachroo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Nimblemind 多智能体系统（nMAS），一种基于证据链接和评分标准的自动化心力衰竭电子健康记录（EHR）特征工程流水线。在500条虚拟患者记录上生成132个结构化特征和70个评分聚合特征，将HFrEF表型分类AUROC从0.895提升至0.963，HFpEF从0.870提升至0.910，并具备审计和证据可追溯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHR特征工程是临床研究和AI领域的主要瓶颈，占数据科学家39-45%的工作量。在心力衰竭领域尤为突出（影响约670万美国成年人），需要整合碎片化EHR数据与疾病特异性、指南引导的临床推理。现有基于规则和LLM的方法仅提供部分自动化，缺乏可维护性和证据可追溯性。

Method: 开发了nMAS（Nimblemind多智能体系统），这是一个证据链接、评分标准引导的自动化心力衰竭特征工程流水线。在来自九个EHR源表的500条虚拟患者记录上进行评估，生成结构化特征和评分聚合特征，并通过受限LLM进行结构完整性、评分标准符合性、来源追溯及审计验证。

Result: nMAS生成了132个结构化特征和70个评分聚合特征；加入聚合特征后，持出集AUROC从0.895提升至0.963（HFrEF）、0.870提升至0.910（HFpEF）；独立的基于LLM的评分评估显示特征在证据支持和方法学严谨性方面获得最高分的81.5%。

Conclusion: 研究证明了在复杂心血管EHR数据上实现自动化、可审计特征工程的可行性。但评估仅限于单机构队列，仍需外部验证以确认泛化性。

Abstract: Electronic health record (EHR) feature engineering is a major bottleneck in clinical research and AI, accounting for 39-45% of data scientists' workload. This is especially pronounced in heart failure, which affects an estimated 6.7 million U.S. adults and requires integrating fragmented EHR data with disease-specific, guideline-based clinical reasoning. Existing rule-based and large language model (LLM)-based approaches offer only partial automation with limited maintainability and evidence traceability. We developed the Nimblemind Multi-Agent System (nMAS), an evidence-linked, rubric-grounded pipeline for automated heart-failure feature engineering, and evaluated it on 500 dummy patient records from nine EHR source tables. nMAS generated 132 structured and 70 rubric-scored aggregated features, verified for structural integrity, rubric compliance, and provenance, and audited by a restricted LLM. Adding the aggregated features improved held-out AUROC from 0.895 to 0.963 for HFrEF and 0.870 to 0.910 for HFpEF phenotyping, and an independent LLM-based rubric assessment of evidence support and methodological soundness scored the features at 81.5% of maximum points. These results demonstrate the feasibility of automated, auditable feature engineering for complex cardiovascular EHR data, though evaluation was limited to a single-institution cohort and external validation is needed.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [288] [Quality Diversity for Reliable Data Driven Time-Use Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05230)
*Aneta Neumann,Ty Stanford,Dorothea Dumuid,Frank Neumann*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将预测不确定性纳入质量多样性（QD）优化的框架，用于更可靠地生成每日时间分配（时间使用构成）建议。利用成分数据分析从超过1000名儿童的大型队列数据中构建目标函数，将不确定性直接嵌入优化过程，使推荐结果在保持高质量结构的同时偏向低不确定性区域，实现更可靠的健康决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每日24小时时间预算的分配与身心健康和认知健康密切相关。现有预测模型虽能估计时间使用构成与健康结果（如BMI、生活满意度、认知能力）之间的关系，但大多数优化方法仅关注最大化预期收益，未考虑数据驱动预测中固有的不确定性。忽略不确定性可能导致不切实际的时间使用建议，因此需要一种能将不确定性纳入优化过程的可靠推荐框架。

Method: 作者构建了一个不确定性量化质量多样性（QD）框架。具体方法包括：(1) 使用成分数据分析（compositional data analysis）从超过1000名儿童的大型队列数据中推导目标函数，刻画日常活动构成与多种健康指标之间的关系；(2) 提出一种将预测不确定性融入QD过程的新方法，在最大化预期健康收益与模型置信度之间取得平衡；(3) 通过基于变量和基于目标两种行为表征方式探索解空间，揭示在不确定性下的多样高质量时间使用构成及健康结果间的显式关系。

Result: 该框架能够产生兼顾预期健康收益与模型置信度的更可靠推荐；通过两种行为表征方式探索解空间，发现了多样化的高质量时间使用构成，并揭示了不确定性条件下健康结果之间的显式关系。将不确定性直接嵌入优化后，推荐结果向低不确定性区域偏移，同时保留了高质量结构。

Conclusion: 通过将不确定性直接嵌入优化过程，该框架使时间使用推荐向低不确定性区域转移，同时保持高质量结构，为行为健康领域提供了更可靠的决策支持。这一方法有效弥合了数据驱动预测与实用化健康建议之间的差距。

Abstract: The daily allocation of the finite 24-hour time budget is strongly associated with physical, mental, and cognitive health. While predictive models can estimate the relationship between time-use compositions and health outcomes such as body mass index, life satisfaction, and cognition, most optimization approaches focus only on maximizing expected benefit and do not consider the uncertainty inherent in data-driven prediction. Ignoring uncertainty in health-related decisions can lead to unrealistic time-use recommendations. To address this gap, we introduce an uncertainty quantification Quality Diversity (QD) framework for a more reliable time-use recommendation. Objective functions are derived using compositional data analysis using a large child cohort dataset n > 1000, to capture the relationship between daily activity compositions and multiple health indicators. We develop a new approach that incorporates predictive uncertainty into QD processes and produces more reliable recommendations that balance the expected health benefits with the confidence of the model. We explore the solution space through variable-based and objective-based behavioral representations, revealing diverse high-quality time-use composition and explicit relationships between health outcomes under uncertainty. By embedding uncertainty directly into optimization, our framework shifts the time-use recommendations toward regions of lower uncertainty while preserving high-quality structures for more reliable decision-making in behavioral health.

</details>


### [289] [A Unified Causal Inference Framework for the Desirability of Outcome Ranking Paradigm in Benefit-Risk Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05244)
*Yuan Feng,Shiyu Shu,Yixin Fang,Ionut Bebu,Toshimitsu Hamasaki,Scott Evans,Guoqing Diao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的协变量校正因果推断框架，用于估计结局排序期望（DOOR）概率，以进行随机试验和观察性研究中的获益-风险评价。该框架将DOOR概率表达为两种治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的雙线性泛函，通过顺序风险集风险估计条件有序分布，并推导出DOOR概率的有效影响函数（EIF）。通过模拟研究比较多种估计方法，结果表明TMLE-SL和CVTMLE-SL在点估计和推断性能上表现最优，并用抗生素耐药性领导小组的多重耐药菌网络数据进行了实例说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床研究中，获益-风险评价至关重要，但现有的因果推断方法在估计DOOR概率时缺乏统一的协变量校正框架。需要一种能够高效、稳健地估计DOOR概率的方法，以支持在存在协变量混杂的情况下对治疗策略进行公平比较和决策。

Method: 将DOOR概率表达为两种治疗策略下边际有序结局分布的雙线性泛函；通过顺序风险集风险（sequential risk-set hazards）估计条件有序分布；推导DOOR概率的有效影响函数（EIF）；在模拟中比较G-computation、归一化IPW、AIPW和TMLE（基于GLM或Super Learner估计干扰函数）；对AIPW-SL和TMLE-SL进行EIF推断（含或不含交叉拟合），并在不同重叠度、处理效应异质性和处理分配设置下评估。

Result: 模拟结果显示TMLE-SL在点估计中表现最强且最一致，AIPW-SL次之；在推断性能评估中，CVTMLE-SL在DOOR尺度偏差、底层有序分布恢复、标准误准确性和置信区间覆盖方面整体表现最佳。方法学通过多重耐药菌网络数据进行了实例验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的协变量校正因果推断框架为DOOR概率的估计提供了统一且高效的解决方案，其中TMLE-SL和CVTMLE-SL分别是在点估计和推断方面的推荐方法，适合用于随机试验和观察性研究中的获益-风险评价。

Abstract: We developed a unified covariate-adjusted causal inference framework for estimating the desirability of outcome ranking (DOOR) probability for benefit-risk evaluation in randomized trials and observational studies. The framework expresses the DOOR probability as a bilinear functional of the marginal ordinal outcome distributions under the two treatment strategies, estimates conditional ordinal distributions through sequential risk-set hazards, and derives the efficient influence function (EIF) of the DOOR probability. The point-estimation simulations compared G-computation, normalized inverse probability weighting (IPW), augmented IPW (AIPW), and targeted maximum likelihood estimation (TMLE), with nuisance functions estimated using generalized linear models or Super Learner (SL). TMLE-SL showed the strongest and most consistent point-estimation performance, with AIPW-SL ranking second. EIF-based inference was then evaluated for AIPW-SL and TMLE-SL, with and without cross-fitting, across settings varying in overlap, treatment-effect heterogeneity, and treatment allocation. CVTMLE-SL showed the strongest overall performance across DOOR-scale bias, recovery of the underlying ordinal distributions, standard-error accuracy, and confidence-interval coverage. We illustrate the methodology using data from the multidrug-resistant organism network of the Antibacterial Resistance Leadership Group.

</details>


### [290] [Deep Generalised Mixed Models: a Novel Neural Network Structure for Analysing Hierarchical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05930)
*Nina van Gerwen,Dimitris Rizopoulos,Manon Hillegers,Loes Keijsers,Sten Willemsen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Deep Generalised Mixed Model (DGMM)的新型神经网络架构，将混合效应模型推广到深度学习框架中，用于分析经验采样法(ESM)收集的纵向数据。该模型通过变分自编码器和贝叶斯数据增强算法进行估计，能够处理高维度的纵向数据、适应灵活的数据分布，并在数据随机缺失(MAR)时提供有效推断。模型应用于GrowIt!青少年情绪研究，结果显示有潜力但存在模型不稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESM数据分析面临两大挑战：传统统计技术难以扩展到高维数据，而标准机器学习方法因缺失数据带来的选择偏差而产生有偏结果。在GrowIt!应用中，青少年因之前的强烈负面情绪而退出研究，导致数据为随机缺失(MAR)类型，标准机器学习方法无法处理这种情况，因此需要开发能够同时兼顾灵活性和有效推断的方法。

Method: 作者开发了一种Deep Generalised Mixed Model (DGMM)，该架构将传统混合效应模型的固定效应和随机效应思想融入深度学习中。模型通过固定效应和随机效应半参数化地灵活建模数据的均值和相关结构。估计过程采用变分自编码器(VAE)的适应版本和贝叶斯数据增强算法，使得模型能够适应服从通用分布的纵向结果，扩展到高维设置，并在MAR缺失下提供有效推断。

Result: 将DGMM应用于GrowIt!研究和多个模拟实验中，结果表明该模型具有应用潜力但性能并非最优，主要原因是模型存在不稳定性问题。

Conclusion: DGMM为ESM数据分析提供了一条结合深度学习和统计建模的新路径，能够处理传统方法难以应对的高维纵向数据与缺失数据问题，但目前模型稳定性仍需改进，以实现更可靠的性能。

Abstract: The experience sampling method (ESM) is a longitudinal research design where participants report their thoughts, emotional states and behaviours multiple times a day. Our work is motivated by such data collected by the GrowIt! app, which was released to investigate daily emotions among adolescents during the COVID-19 pandemic. Current procedures to analyse ESM data face various challenges. While standard statistical techniques may not scale well to a high-dimensional setting, machine learning procedures can give biased results due to selection bias introduced by missingness. In our motivating dataset, adolescents dropped out due to previous strong feelings of negative emotions. Hence, the implied missing data are of the missing-at-random type that standard machine learning procedures cannot accommodate. We develop a novel neural network architecture that generalises mixed effects models to deep learning to overcome these challenges. It allows semi-parametric and flexible modelling of data's mean and correlation structure through fixed and random effects. For estimation, we use an adaptation of variational auto-encoders and a Bayesian data augmentation algorithm. Through this approach, the model can accommodate longitudinal outcomes following generic distributions, scale well to high-dimensional settings and provide valid inference when data are missing-at-random. We applied the Deep Generalised Mixed Model to the GrowIt! study and various simulations. The results show potential for the Deep Generalised Mixed Model, yet suboptimal performance due to model instability.

</details>


### [291] [Handling Missing Data in Probabilistic Regression Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06195)
*Taiane Schaedler Prass,Alisson Silva Neimaier,Guilherme Pumi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文扩展了概率回归树（PRTree）框架，使其能够在树构建过程中直接处理缺失预测变量，无需事先插补。提出了三种处理缺失值的策略，并验证了在缺失比例较高的真实数据集上，这些方法常优于经典CART回归树，同时保持树模型的解释性和灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典回归树在处理缺失预测变量时需要事先插补，而概率回归树（PRTree）虽能产生连续预测并具有平滑一致性，但尚未支持在树构建期间直接处理缺失值。作者希望在保留PRTree概率特性的前提下，扩展其框架以原生处理缺失数据，避免插补带来的偏差和额外步骤。

Method: 提出三种处理缺失值的策略：(1) 均匀概率方法（uniform-probability）、(2) 部分观测方法（partial-observation）、(3) 降维平滑方法（dimension-reduced smoothing）。这些方法分别以不同方式利用可用信息，并通过设计保持概率守恒（probability conservation）和边际相容性（marginal compatibility）等PRTree的基本概率特性，适用于任意缺失模式。

Result: 在多个具有不同缺失程度的真实数据集上，与经典回归树CART比较，结果表明：fill（填充）策略是影响预测性能的主导组件，其影响甚至超过平滑分布或代理选择准则；在缺失比例较高的数据集中，所提方法经常优于CART。

Conclusion: 概率回归树可直接在构建过程中处理缺失预测变量而无需插补，缺失处理的策略选择对预测性能有显著影响，尤其在数据缺失严重时优于经典CART，且保持树模型的解释性与灵活性。

Abstract: Probabilistic Regression Trees (PRTrees) are a smooth and consistent alternative to classical regression trees, producing continuous predictions through probabilistic split assignments. This paper extends the PRTree framework to accommodate missing predictor values directly during tree construction, eliminating the need for prior imputation. Three strategies are proposed, each exploiting the available information differently: a uniform-probability approach, a partial-observation approach, and a dimension-reduced smoothing approach. These modifications are defined to preserve the fundamental probabilistic properties of the original methodology, including probability conservation and marginal compatibility, under arbitrary patterns of missing covariate values. The proposed methods are evaluated on several real-world datasets exhibiting different levels of missingness and are compared with classical regression trees. The results show that the effectiveness of probabilistic tree construction depends strongly on the treatment of missing observations. Across the considered datasets, the fill strategy emerged as the dominant modeling component, often exerting a larger influence on predictive performance than either the smoothing distribution or the proxy-selection criterion. In datasets where a substantial proportion of observations contained missing predictor values, the proposed methods frequently outperformed CART, while maintaining the interpretability and flexibility of tree-based models.

</details>


### [292] [Beyond Marginal Validity: Finite-Sample Guarantees for Localized Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06206)
*Anton Conrad,Rustam Isaev,Denis Belomestny,Eric Moulines,Sergey Samsonov*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文为随机局部化保形预测（RLCP）提供了首个有限样本高概率保证，联合控制条件覆盖缺口与相对于oracle的长度误差，并刻画了带宽偏差-方差权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保形预测的边际覆盖有效性可能掩盖严重的协变量特定失准，而精确的无分布条件覆盖在有限样本下无法实现。RLCP通过在测试点附近校准来缓解这一差距，但已有理论缺乏对实际实现的局部化集合进行有限样本保证，无法联合控制条件有效性与oracle效率。

Method: 在固定的评分函数下，假设条件评分CDF满足Hölder正则性以及标准密度与核假设，证明了在实现的局部化邻域上均匀成立的条件覆盖缺口和相对于oracle的长度误差的高概率界；界分解为O(h^β)的局部化偏差和随校准规模递减的校准项；对数据划分学习评分（如分位回归保形），统一局部保证分解为固定评分校准项与评分估计误差项。

Result: 推导出的界清晰地刻画了带宽偏差-方差权衡，说明了RLCP何时能追踪oracle表现；对于指向枢轴评分的已学习评分，证明改进的学习可锐化局部化保证。

Conclusion: 本文填补了RLCP理论空白，提供了联合控制条件有效性与oracle效率的有限样本保证，为实践中的带宽选择和评分学习提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Conformal prediction endows arbitrary black-box predictors with finite-sample, distribution-free marginal coverage, yet marginal validity can hide severe covariate-specific miscalibration, while exact distribution-free conditional coverage is finite-sample unattainable. Randomly localized conformal prediction (RLCP) mitigates this gap by calibrating near the test point while preserving marginal coverage. Existing theory, however, lacks finite-sample guarantees for the realized localized set that jointly control conditional validity and oracle efficiency. We provide such guarantees. For any fixed score, under Hölder regularity of the conditional score CDF and standard density and kernel assumptions, we prove high-probability bounds, uniform over a realized localization neighbourhood, for the conditional-coverage gap and the length error relative to the oracle. The bounds decompose into an $O(h^β)$ localization bias and a calibration term decreasing with calibration size, clarifying the bandwidth bias-variance tradeoff and when RLCP tracks the oracle. We also analyze data-split learned scores: when the score targets a pivotal score, as in conformalized quantile regression, uniform local guarantees decompose into fixed-score calibration and uniform score-estimation errors, showing that improved learning sharpens localized guarantees.

</details>


### [293] [Minimax Optimal Early-Stopped Gradient Descent for Gaussian Mixture Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06250)
*Alex Buna,Shirley Xiaoqi Liu,Patrick Rebeschini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了过参数化分类中梯度下降的隐式偏差导致的统计次优性，证明在带标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型下，适当时间的早停可使GD达到极小极大最优的过零一风险，优于线性插值器（后者需要指数级更多的样本）。核心贡献是新的校准结果，将过拟逻辑风险转化为过零一风险，消除了标准界限中的平方根率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在过参数化分类中，训练数据即使底层分布不可分也可能是线性可分的。此时逻辑损失上的梯度下降在范数上发散但方向上收敛到最大间隔插值分类器，其隐式偏差在统计上可能是次优的。作者希望证明早停可以克服这种次优性，弥补现有理论中插值分类器统计性能不佳的局限。

Method: 论文在带标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型下分析GD早停算法。方法包括：(1) 对早停迭代器建立尖锐的上界；(2) 通过匹配的统计下界（对任意分类器）确定极小极大最优率；(3) 核心技术贡献是一个新的校准结果，将过拟逻辑风险转换为过零一风险，该结果能够处理标签翻转噪声引起的模型误设定，并去除了标准界限中的平方根率；(4) 还在理论上建立了线性插值器的下界，证明其样本复杂度远高于早停。

Result: (1) 早停的GD在具有快速连续衰减的协方差谱（包括多项式和指数衰减）下达到极小极大最优的过零一风险；(2) 新校准结果消除了logistic风险到0-1风险转换中的平方根率，处理了模型误设定问题；(3) 线性插值器实现相同过零一风险需要指数级更多的样本，验证了早停的统计优势；(4) 理论速率通过实验得到验证。

Conclusion: 早停是克服过参数化GD隐式偏差统计次优性的有效手段。在标签翻转噪声的高斯混合模型下，早停能够实现极小极大最优的分类风险，而线性插值则可能需要指数级更多的样本。该工作为实践中早停正则化的有效性提供了理论依据，并给出了新的风险转化工具来处理模型误设定。

Abstract: In overparameterised classification, training data can be linearly separable even when the underlying distribution is not. In this setting, gradient descent (GD) on the logistic loss diverges in norm while converging in direction to a max-margin interpolating classifier, whose implicit bias can be statistically suboptimal. In this work, we show that early stopping can overcome this suboptimality: in a Gaussian mixture model with label-flipping noise, GD stopped at an appropriate oracle time achieves minimax-optimal excess zero-one risk for covariance spectra with fast and continuous decay, including polynomial and exponential spectral decays. Our analysis combines a sharp upper bound for the early-stopped iterate with a matching statistical lower bound over arbitrary classifiers, yielding optimal rates that are validated by experiments. A central technical contribution is a new calibration result that converts excess logistic risk into excess zero-one risk; it handles the model misspecification induced by the label-flipping noise, and removes the square-root rate in standard bounds. We also establish a lower bound for linear interpolators, showing that interpolation can require exponentially more samples than early stopping to achieve the same excess risk.

</details>


### [294] [Stochastic Dynamics on Persistence Diagram Space via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06276)
*Farzana Nasrin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于持久性图（PD）空间上随机动力学的强化学习框架，通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作定义可控马尔可夫过程，并证明其马尔可夫链的不可约性、非周期性与几何遍历性，从而保证PD空间上唯一平稳概率测度的存在。通过设计包含分布匹配、拓扑统计目标与结构保持压缩的奖励函数，实现对科学相关拓扑目标的自适应简化与概率建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久性图是捕捉多尺度拓扑结构的稳定且可解释的工具，但其统计建模与随机演化框架尚不完善。现有文献通常将PD视为静态对象，缺乏对PD空间上概率建模和随机演化的统一框架，难以支持自适应拓扑简化与概率性推断。

Method: 引入强化学习框架来建模PD空间上的随机动力学。核心思路包括：(1) 通过拓扑感知的局部编辑操作定义图的演化；(2) 在可变基数的有限PD空间上构造受控马尔可夫过程；(3) 证明马尔可夫链的不可约性、非周期性和几何遍历性，确保唯一平稳分布的存在；(4) 设计融合分布匹配、任务特定拓扑统计量和结构保持压缩的多目标奖励函数，引导动力学向目标拓扑结构演化。

Result: 在合成数据与神经影像持久性图上进行实验，结果表明所提出框架能够保持主要拓扑结构的同时有效降低图的复杂度，验证了框架在自适应拓扑简化与概率建模方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为PD空间上的随机动力学提供了理论严格的强化学习框架，兼具理论保证（遍历性与唯一平稳分布）与实际效能（结构保真与复杂度降低），为拓扑数据分析中的动态建模和概率推断开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Persistence diagrams (PDs) provide stable and interpretable summaries of multiscale topological structure. While substantial progress has been made in the statistical analysis of PDs, existing literature often treats diagrams as static objects and provide limited frameworks for probabilistic modeling and stochastic evolution on PD space. We introduce a reinforcement learning framework for stochastic dynamics on PD space, where diagrams evolve through topology aware local edit operations. The dynamics define controlled Markov processes on spaces of finite PDs with variable cardinality. We establish conditions under which the induced Markov chains are irreducible, aperiodic, and geometrically ergodic, implying the existence of unique stationary probability laws on PD space. To guide the dynamics toward scientifically relevant topological targets, we formulate objectives that encompass distribution matching, task specific topological statistics, and structure-preserving compression. The resulting rewards balance task specific distributional targets, diagram fidelity, and complexity reduction, and yield a framework for adaptive topological simplification and probabilistic modeling. Experiments on synthetic and neuroimaging PDs demonstrate that the proposed framework can preserve dominant topological structure while reducing diagram complexity.

</details>


### [295] [Optimal Rates for Learning with Monotone Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06337)
*Anay Mehrotra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究单调对抗者注入额外正确标注样本时的学习问题。结果表明，当VC维≥2时，额外对数代价是内在的：极小极大期望误差为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))，而当d=1时为Θ(1/n)。同样的结果适用于Littlestone维度，表明在线到批次的O(d_L/n)速率在此模型下不可达。维度一的上界由改进的留一法分析实现，所有下界来自单一构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Larsen等人在提出的单调对抗插入模型中，展示了经验风险最小化达到O((d/n)log(n/d))的期望误差，且已知的最优学习器都会被推离PAC最优的Θ(d/n)速率。他们留下了一个开放问题：额外的对数因子是特定算法的局限，还是缺乏可交换性这一根本特性的内在后果。本文旨在回答这个关键问题。

Method: 作者证明了额外的对数代价在VC维≥2时是内在的（基于最坏情况的极小极大分析），并为d=1构造了简单的不当学习器达到Θ(1/n)上界，其分析改编自一包含图（one-inclusion graph）的留一法论证。所有下界均来自一个统一的显式构造：在一个先验分布上，两个在非平凡质量点上不同的目标假设产生相同的样本，从而限制任何学习器的误差。

Result: 极小极大期望误差在d=1时为Θ(1/n)，在d≥2时为Θ((d/n)log(n/d))。同样的速率在将VC维替换为Littlestone维度时也成立，表明在线学习的有限错误界并不能保证在此对抗插入模型中获得O(d_L/n)的批次学习速率。维度一的上界由简单的不当学习器实现。

Conclusion: 该结果解决了Larsen等人的开放问题：对数代价是根本性的，而非算法伪影。反直觉地，即使所有插入例子都被正确标注，单调对抗者仍可使学习变难一个对数因子。这揭示了在缺乏可交换性时，VC维和Littlestone维度下的批次学习速率会发生根本性退化。

Abstract: A monotone adversary observes an i.i.d. labeled sample and appends a finite number of further examples of its choice, every one of them labeled correctly by the target hypothesis. The learner sees a uniform shuffle of the combined sample and is scored on the original distribution. Every example is correctly labeled, but the insertions depend on the clean sample, so the combined sample is not exchangeable. Larsen, Pabbaraju, and Shetty, who introduced this model, showed that empirical risk minimization attains expected error $O((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for classes of VC dimension $d$, and that every known optimal learner can be pushed away from the $Θ(d/n)$ rate, optimal for PAC learning. They asked whether the extra logarithm is an artifact of those particular algorithms or an inherent consequence of the lack of exchangeability.
  We show that this additional cost is inherent beyond VC dimension one. In the worst case over classes of VC dimension $d$ and over known finite insertion budgets, the minimax expected error is $Θ(1/n)$ at $d=1$ and $Θ((d/n)\log(n/d))$ for $d\geq 2$. The same rates hold with Littlestone dimension $d_{\mathrm L}$ in place of $d$, so the clean online-to-batch rate $O(d_{\mathrm L}/n)$ is unattainable as well. Thus, somewhat counterintuitively, adding correctly labeled examples can make learning harder by a logarithmic factor, even for classes that admit finite mistake bounds in online learning. The dimension-one upper bound is achieved by a simple improper learner whose analysis adapts the leave-one-out argument underlying the one-inclusion graph. All of our lower bounds are elementary and come from a single construction: an explicit class and prior on which two target hypothesis, which differ a point of nonnegligible mass, produce the same sample.

</details>


### [296] [Scalable estimation of VARMA models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06340)
*Daniel Paulin,Victor Elvira*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的向量自回归移动平均(VARMA)模型估计框架，通过部分自相关重参数化、高斯先验和Parseval（傅里叶）恒等式实现每次优化迭代的计算复杂度与序列长度T无关，使VARMA模型在中等维度（d=10到40）下的估计变得实用，且恢复无限自回归表示的速度接近参数速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VARMA模型在中等维度以上一直被认为不实用，主要障碍包括：似然函数非凸、参数化仅在等价类下可识别、每次似然评估都需要遍历整个序列。然而VARMA的移动平均项可以用少量参数捕捉纯自回归需要很多滞后才能匹配的动力学特征。本文旨在移除这一计算障碍，使VARMA估计在实际问题规模下可行。

Method: 该框架包含三个关键组件：(1) 部分自相关重参数化，从构造上保证平稳性和可逆性；(2) 对重参数化系数施加高斯先验，对角与非对角项使用不同的尺度参数；(3) 损失函数仅依赖固定大小的充分统计量，通过Parseval（傅里叶）恒等式以近线性成本（相对于截断长度）评估。由此产生两个点估计器：正则化最小二乘拟合和协方差边缘化的最大后验估计器。该机制还扩展至季节性动态、外生回归变量（VARMAX）和滚动窗口重拟合。

Result: 理论证明在固定维度下，两个估计器均以接近参数的速率恢复真实过程的无限自回归表示，截断不引入渐近偏差。实验上，从d=10到d=40，估计器与预言机预测误差保持接近（而经典条件MLE在此范围返回不可逆的拟合导致预测发散），并在零售需求、气象和空气质量数据集上匹敌或超过VAR、贝叶斯VAR、分量-wise ARMA和稀疏VARMA基线。

Conclusion: 该框架将基于似然的VARMA估计的每次迭代成本实现为与序列长度无关，使VARMA建模在以往只能依赖VAR模型的问题规模下变得可行，填补了VARMA方法在实际应用中的计算空白。

Abstract: Vector autoregressive moving-average (VARMA) models have long been considered impractical beyond moderate dimensions: the likelihood is non-convex, the parametrization is identified only up to equivalence, and every evaluation costs a pass over the entire series. Yet their moving-average term captures with a few parameters what a pure autoregression matches only with many lags. We introduce an estimation framework that removes this computational barrier: each optimization iteration is independent of the series length $T$. The framework combines a partial-autocorrelation reparametrization that guarantees stationarity and invertibility by construction, Gaussian priors on the reparametrized coefficients with separate scales for diagonal and off-diagonal entries, and losses that depend on the data only through fixed-size sufficient statistics, evaluated by a Parseval (Fourier) identity at near-linear cost in the truncation length. This yields two point estimators: a regularized least-squares fit and a covariance-marginalized maximum-a-posteriori estimator. We prove that both recover the infinite-autoregressive representation of the true process at a near-parametric rate in fixed dimension, so the truncation introduces no asymptotic bias. The same machinery extends, at the same leading cost, to seasonal dynamics, exogenous regressors (VARMAX), and rolling-window refits. Empirically, the estimators stay close to the oracle forecast error from $d=10$ to $d=40$ (where classical conditional MLE returns non-invertible fits whose forecasts diverge) and match or beat VAR, Bayesian-VAR, component-wise ARMA, and sparse-VARMA baselines on retail-demand, meteorological, and air-quality data. This brings likelihood-based VARMA estimation, at a per-iteration cost independent of the series length, to the problem sizes where practitioners have so far relied on VAR models.

</details>
